# The transition from ordinary physics to modern algebra: a dated, cited history of every point where a thing of physics became an object of the algebra

The canonical statement of this algebra is [docs/ALGEBRA.md](../../ALGEBRA.md), section 7 (the steps) and 2.10 (the quantities); this file is kept as the record of 2026-09-22.

The Transition Historian, 2026-09-22, on the model owner's order of that
day (about 06:10Z, by voice, relayed by the Boss): "This is the basis of
the paper. What we must explain in the paper is how we got from ordinary
physics to algebraic mathematics: when we made that transition. It is in
main and in the code: the transition from when we showed that ordinary
physics behaves like modern algebra. From the beginning of the paper to
the end, to the formulas, one only needs to show WHY physics behaves like
modern algebra, otherwise one is just showing formulas in modern algebra.
Explain why physics behaves like modern algebra, with the couplings and
everything we did; it is all in the code and in main. It must be shown in
the paper."

This file is a history, not a rule and not a hypothesis. It adds no
physics and no formula of its own. Every entry is one transition, in date
order, and states (a) the thing as ordinary physics names it; (b) the
object it became in the law; (c) when, and by whose word, cited to the
Highlights line or the log record, the owner's words quoted where the
record has them (every quotation of the owner is a translation, as the
records mark it); (d) where it lives in the code on `main` today, as
file and line; (e) the reading that showed ordinary physics behaving like
it, a DETECTOR reading with its NATURE row where one exists, and a
GAMEBOARD number named so and never compared with nature; (f) what was
superseded, and by what, where a line was later replaced. A historical
model is named as history and never as the active law. The law in force
is the Beam Law, `beam-v1` ([HIGHLIGHTS 5.4](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#54-the-detector));
the readings' kinds are the register's
([TERMINOLOGY, the readings](../../TERMINOLOGY.md#the-readings)).

braically-whatever-can-be-the-board-being-finite-and-computable-a-run-proves-what-the-algebra-cannot-the-moving-detector-read-as-the-transponding-body-of-the-click-frames-missing-direction-a-record-that-carries-its-momentum-node-to-node-and-re-emits-what-it-receives-its-own-count-read-at-the-ground-through-its-records-the-one-run-that-decides-the-ratio-of-the-two-one-way-factors-put-to-the-owner-in-the-six-lines-for-his-word)).

Notation ([skills/workflow.md, "Notation"](../../../skills/workflow.md#notation-every-symbol-named-its-kind-shown-the-model-owner-2026-09-21-record-184)):
a scalar plain (N the steps of the phase circle, Q the label's scale, M a
body's content), a vector in bold lowercase (**p** the momentum vector,
**f** a record's phase-count vector, **s** the state vector on the torus,
**r** its rate vector), a matrix or an operator in bold uppercase (**C**
the coupling matrix, **G** the click's Gram matrix, **E** the tables'
matrix, **F** the interval's map, **R** the reading, **T** the traceless
second-moment tensor). A Greek letter is written as a word and named at
its first use (gamma, the Lorentz factor; zeta_N, the primitive N-th root
of unity; rho, the charge per unit of content).

## The entries, in date order

### 1. 2026-09-17: the shared quantum resource is deleted; locality holds without exception

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The non-local correlation of a
  measured pair (Bell's test), which the earlier engine served through a
  shared quantum resource, `Q-ORACLE-1`, an owner that answered at a
  distance.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** Nothing: the resource is deleted,
  and every update of every Node reads only its own record and the six
  causally available neighbours ([LOCALITY-1](../../../SIMULATOR_DEFINITIONS.md#locality-1-end-to-end-local-physics),
  "There is no model-computation exception"). What was a shared
  resource became, in the end, one more thing on the GameBoard read at
  one Node: the record's click (entry 12 below).
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** 2026-09-17, the model owner: section
  3.18 of the Highlights, [deleted on 2026-09-17](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#318-deleted-on-2026-09-17),
  "because it contradicts the Detector of section 3.19: no owner answers
  at a distance ... and ordinary locality holds without exception. The
  accepted price is that two Detectors at equal distance from the birth
  draw independently (CHSH at most 2 for spacelike settings)." The
  deletion in the tree: [MIGRATION, "Shared quantum resource and integration layer deleted on 2026-09-17"](../../MIGRATION.md#shared-quantum-resource-and-integration-layer-deleted-on-2026-09-17);
  the historical contract kept as history in
  [SIMULATOR_DEFINITIONS, Q-ORACLE-1 (deleted)](../../../SIMULATOR_DEFINITIONS.md#historical-opt-in-quantum-contracts--q-oracle-1-deleted-on-2026-09-17).
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** In the absence of any such
  path: the architecture gate rejects world or replay member access in a
  physical calculation and the six-read tests check the neighbour
  boundary (`tests/test_architecture.py`, named in the LOCALITY-1
  contract); the one engine's interval reads a Node's arrivals and the
  six Ports only (`src/event_universe/events/nature_beam.py:3299`,
  `nature_beam`, and the walk at `:3966`).
- **(e) The reading.** The price was read exactly as stated: under the
  crowd form's window Bell's S (the CHSH sum) reads 2 exactly with every
  marginal 1/2 (DETECTOR; [record 71](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#71-measured-363-bell-with-the-choosers-on-the-gameboard); NATURE row 1b, FAIL against the
  measured 2.42 as the local bound). What replaced the deleted resource
  is the record's click, whose S = 176/64 = 2.75 (DETECTOR; NATURE row 1a,
  PASS at 1.65 standard errors of Hensen et al. 2015) is entry 12's
  reading; the two rows stand side by side in
  [NATURE, the rows](../../NATURE.md#the-rows).
- **(f) Superseded.** The ray-event model of 2026-09-17 (a Detector's bit,
  a draw of 1 or 0, a return that walks back) that took the resource's
  place was itself superseded by the law of the bit (2026-09-18), the law
  of the shadow (the evening of 2026-09-18), the law of events and the law
  of the ray (2026-09-19): [HIGHLIGHTS 5.4, decisions of 2026-09-17 and 2026-09-18](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-17-and-2026-09-18-superseded-on-2026-09-19-by-the-law-of-the-ray).
  The deletion itself was never reversed.

### 2. 2026-09-17: the amplitude is a phase and a conserved content; Born's rule is a coupling, a declared table of bounded integer ratios

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The quantum amplitude (a complex
  number), the probability of a click as its square, and interference.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "There is no amplitude as a number
  and no probability field: the phase and the ray's conserved content
  together are the discrete stand-in for the quantum amplitude, the phase
  as its angle and the conserved content as its size"; interference is
  steering by the phase difference; "the basic steering coupling is the
  Born rule stated as a coupling: for a phase difference delta it splits
  the shared content between the two candidate Ports in the ratio
  cos^2(delta/2) to sin^2(delta/2), as a declared table of bounded
  integer ratios with the remainder owned ... It is declared, not
  derived" ([HIGHLIGHTS 3.3, "Amplitude and interference"](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#33-ray-form-propagation-is-fundamental)).
  The phase is an integer step of a circle of N steps (the family's phase
  width N), a residue modulo N.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** 2026-09-17, the model owner, Highlights
  3.3 as taken into git on 2026-09-17 (the head of
  [HIGHLIGHTS](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#universe24-highlights): "By the model
  owner's decision of 2026-09-17 this file is edited directly").
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The phase as a residue
  modulo N: `src/event_universe/core/phase.py:119` (`PhaseCircle`, "the
  cyclic group of the phase"); the row's `phase` column,
  `src/event_universe/events/nature_beam.py:216` (`NatureBeam`). The
  declared table is gone (see (f)); its descendant is the table from N of
  entry 4 and the Gram form of entry 17.
- **(e) The reading.** None under this form: the runs of 2026-09-17 are
  historical (Highlights section 6, "Bell results recorded with the
  former shared resource ... are historical"). The first registered
  interference readings came under the amplitude law (entry 12).
- **(f) Superseded.** On 2026-09-18 by "the table is computed from the
  family's phase width N and declared by no one" (entry 4); on
  2026-09-19 by the law of the ray, where the Node holds no wave and the
  click reads the square of the coherent sum (entry 7); on 2026-09-21 by
  the click without amplitudes, one bilinear form on the record (entry
  17). What survived every replacement is the pair: the phase as an
  integer of Z_N and the amount as a conserved integer, on the row's
  record.

### 3. 2026-09-17: a force is a catalog entry read at a meeting; the engine performs only simple operations

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The forces (gravity, the electric
  force, the strong and the weak interaction) and polarization, as
  mechanisms of a dynamics.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "Forces and polarization are catalog
  entries, not engine mechanisms": "The engine performs only the simple
  operations: a step on a Link, a phase advance, a split by a declared
  table, a sum, and the one draw at a marked Node. Anything that does not
  change how a ray moves between events is therefore a family property in
  the catalog or a coupling table, read only at a meeting, exactly as
  charge is" ([HIGHLIGHTS 3.26](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#326-from-symmetry-groups-to-states-and-interactions-on-the-board)).
  This is the first list of the engine's verbs and the first statement
  that a force is a table and not a code path.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** Approved 2026-09-17 (the paragraph's
  own date, Highlights 3.26).
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The family table:
  `src/event_universe/events/world.py:763` (`FamilyDefinition`: quantum
  h, charge rho, columns, lifetime L, hand); the columns as the one
  coupling, entry 9. The engine branches on no force's name
  ([AGENTS.md, change boundaries](../../../AGENTS.md#change-boundaries)).
- **(e) The reading.** None of its own on 2026-09-17; the readings of the
  columns are entry 9's.
- **(f) Superseded.** The five operations of this paragraph (with "the
  one draw at a marked Node") were superseded by the six verbs of the law
  with no draw (entry 16, [record 181](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#181-decided-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0110z-the-owners-words-translated-so-the-system-becomes-vectors-tensors-and-operations-on-them-where-the-gameboard-checks-and-computes-when-there-is-a-limit-and-put-it-in-highlights-and-why-it-is-right-the-statement-the-state-is-integer-vectors-on-tori-a-row-a-record-a-bodys-counts-and-one-tensor-the-readings-order-2-moment-the-law-one-map-f-per-interval-at-every-node-made-of-six-operations-a-translation-by-a-rate-with-a-threshold-a-multiplication-by-a-declared-matrix-an-addition-an-inner-product-a-permutation-an-evaluation-and-the-gameboard-computes-exactly-where-the-formula-has-only-a-limit-a-constant-rate-gives-the-closed-form-floors_0--r-t-and-the-board-only-confirms-it-a-state-dependent-rate-gives-a-difference-equation-with-no-closed-form-whose-exact-solution-is-the-boards-iteration-and-whose-limit-is-the-derivations-differential-equation-the-measurement-the-one-threshold-read-out-why-it-holds-1-the-inventory-of-every-rule-of-beam-v1-finds-each-one-of-the-six-operations-or-a-declared-rounding-at-load-record-168-so-the-statement-is-a-fact-of-the-code-not-a-hope-2-the-two-steps-that-look-like-exceptions-are-not-the-euclidean-division-with-the-remainder-kept-is-a-bijection-z--zd-x-z-whose-carry-is-the-event-the-definition-of-a-whole-quantum-record-173-and-the-clicks-weight-is-the-inner-product-of-the-pointer-with-itself-through-the-couplings-primitive-record-173-so-nothing-is-squared-as-a-step-of-its-own-3-the-constant-rate-closed-form-reproduces-the-register-exactly-the-bell-cells-by-the-papers-s_of_n-the-cones-pins-before-its-run-the-64-registered-clicks-of-slits_low-by-the-map-record-156-so-computed-before-the-run-is-verified-4-the-feedback-class-has-no-closed-form-in-principle-the-rate-reads-the-state-so-the-iteration-is-the-only-exact-solution-and-the-boards-necessity-is-a-theorem-while-the-continuum-limit-is-the-derivations-and-the-pairing-checks-both-records-167-176-5-it-is-the-division-nat and [record the draw by the
  wheel (entry 18). The principle, a force as a table, stands as the
  columns (entry 9).

### 4. 2026-09-18: the Born table is computed from the phase width N and declared by no one; a clock is content; a Node is its six Ports

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** Three things of one day. (i) The
  Born rule's numbers, until then a declared table. (ii) A clock and a
  mass: the rest rate of a family's phase. (iii) The memory a Node keeps
  (registers, remainders, traces).
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** (i) "The steering table is not a
  declared input ...: it is the integer geometry of the phase circle,
  computed once from the family's phase width N, the share that continues
  at a difference of d steps being the squared length of the sum of two
  unit phases d steps apart, cos^2(pi d / N), taken in N-ths and rounded
  to the nearest whole ... No world declares its numbers and no catalog
  entry names them: ... the only input behind interference is N"
  ([record 12](../../LOG_2026-09-18.md#12-17-a-shadow-spreads-by-its-phase), point 17). (ii) "A thing's clock is its content": "a thing
  at rest advances its phase by content / K steps per interval, with one
  universal K for every family, so mass, clock and computation are one
  number", and E = h nu (E the energy, h Planck's constant, nu the
  frequency) "is conservation of amount together with this rule and h is
  K in lattice units" ([record 14](../../LOG_2026-09-18.md#14-19-a-things-clock-is-its-content), point 19). (iii) "A Node is its six
  Ports; everything else is a ray": every store kept on a Node becomes a
  ray with a property and "the word register leaves the model with the
  stores it named" ([record 17](../../LOG_2026-09-18.md#17-22-a-node-is-its-six-ports-everything-else-is-a-ray), point 22).
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-18, the law
  of the bit ([record 7](../../LOG_2026-09-18.md#07-the-law-of-the-bit-the-thing-or-its-shadow), points 15 to 25 as [record 12](../../LOG_2026-09-18.md), [record 14](../../LOG_2026-09-18.md) and
  [record 17](../../LOG_2026-09-18.md) record them); the Highlights line
  [decisions of 2026-09-17 and 2026-09-18](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-17-and-2026-09-18-superseded-on-2026-09-19-by-the-law-of-the-ray).
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** (i) The tables of the
  circle computed once per N from integer series and never declared:
  `src/event_universe/core/phase.py:97` (`phase_cosines`, "cos(2 pi d / P)
  x 256, rounded to the nearest integer"), `:82` (`phase_sines`), the
  scale `PHASE_COSINE_SCALE = 256` at `:21`; read at the click through
  the Gram matrix (`:61`, `phase_gram`; entry 17). (ii) The turn of a
  body's clock at the rate content x n against the wall d, one row of the
  counts table: `src/event_universe/events/world.py:1161`
  (`NatureBeamWorld.turn`) and `src/event_universe/events/measured.py:379`
  (`counts_table`); the constant-rate identity `by_clock(age, content,
  K)` at `src/event_universe/core/integer.py:60`. (iii) Nothing at a
  Node: the store is the host's arrays sorted by Node
  (`src/event_universe/events/nature_beam.py:1883`, `NatureBeamStore`),
  every count an accumulator on a body's record (entry 14), the third
  test of every rule, local.
- **(e) The reading.** (i) The tables' rounding is read at every click:
  Malus's law at 45 and 90 degrees exact (128 of 256, 0 of 256, 64 of
  256; DETECTOR; NATURE row 9, PASS) and at 22.5 degrees 219/256 =
  0.85547 against cos^2(22.5 degrees) = 0.85355, "the +0.0019 the
  tables' rounding at the scale 256 (the owner's declared input, record
  328), engine-exact" (DETECTOR, NATURE row 9's note). (ii) E = h f
  under its later form is entry 8's. (iii) The absence of a register is
  read by the byte-identity of the gate set at every landing (the
  register's replay, [TERMINOLOGY, gate set](../../TERMINOLOGY.md#the-readings)).
- **(f) Superseded.** The law of the bit was superseded the same evening
  by the law of the shadow ([record 29](../../LOG_2026-09-18.md#29-the-law-of-the-shadow-only-shadows-and-events): "No real and shadow. There is
  only shadow. There are events, which are a whole quantum."), and that
  on 2026-09-19 by the law of events and the law of the ray (entry 6).
  What stood through every law: the table from N (kept on 2026-09-21 as
  the rounded tables at 1/256, "the existing solution", the owner,
  [record 328](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#328-the-owners-correction-of-record-327-the-beautiful-solution-is-solution-1-the-rounded-cosine-table-stays-as-a-declared-input-of-the-law-the-precedents-2026-09-21-about-0912z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-0920z-the-owner-translated-it-is-the-existing-solution-no-check-famous-papers-that-were-like-this-with-solution-1-the-bosss-reading-of-record-327-the-exact-ring-exact-circle-v1-withdrawn-the-derivation-mathematicians-seventh-order-stopped-before-any-file-nothing-changes-on-the-tree-decided-corephasepys-tables-of-cos-and-sin-at-1256-computed-once-at-load-from-integer-series-law-data-and-not-a-register-at-a-node-read-only-at-the-click-through-the-gram-matrix-g--et-e-of-record-188-stay-as-they-are-and-are-declared-an-input-of-the-law-beside-q-s-and-n-the-circles-rounding-one-line-in-the-papers-inputs-and-in-terminologys-widths-on-its-next-edit-the-precedents-found-by-the-boss-famous-work-that-declared-a-rounding-of-the-circle-as-its-definition-and-kept-it-1-malvar-hallapuro-karczewicz-and-kerofsky-2003-ieee-trans-circuits-syst-video-technol-13-low-complexity-transform-and-quantization-in-h264avc-the-cosines-of-the-dct-replaced-by-small-integers-the-transform-computed-exactly-in-16-bit-integer-arithmetic-the-rounding-made-the-standard-so-that-every-decoder-gives-the-same-bits-no-inverse-transform-mismatch-the-closest-analogue-2-welch-1969-ieee-trans-audio-electroacoust-17-151-a-fixed-point-fast-fourier-transform-error-analysis-the-rounded-factors-of-the-fixed-point-fft-the-rms-error-bounded-above-and-below-the-rounding-kept-and-bounded-not-removed-3-mathews-1963-science-142-553-the-digital-computer-as-a-musical-instrument-a-stored-waveform-table-read-repetitively-at-a-rate-the-table-lookup-oscillator-a-rounded-sine-table-as-the-definition-of-the-tone-the-norm-of-every-synthesizer-since-4-goodman-and-silvestri-1970-ibm-j-res-dev-14-478-some-effects-of-fourier-domain-phase-quantization-the-phase-quantized-to-n-equally-spaced-levels-computer-holog the clock as a count of the body's own phase steps (the

  The shadow's mixing at a Node and the parked remainder are history
  ([MIGRATION, the law of the shadow](../../MIGRATION.md#the-law-of-the-shadow-a-new-engine-mode-on-2026-09-18-field-only-v1)).

### 5. 2026-09-19: the law of events: "Everything is derived from vector operations"; a field is an event; matter is a measured event

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A field, matter, a register of
  memory, a single quantum that spreads.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** Only events: "Let the assumption be
  that there are no registers. Everything is derived from vector
  operations and the like"; "A single quantum simply passes in one
  direction ... because of it, it no longer spreads"; "There are no
  fields; a field is an event"; "There is no matter either. Matter ..."
  is a measured event ([record 2](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md#02-the-law-of-events-no-fields-no-matter-no-registers-only-events), the owner's words). The word "vector
  operations" enters the law here, on 2026-09-19, before any formula was
  written in that form.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-19
  ([record 2](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md); the principles as he gave them, fifteen, checked against
  the decisions, [record 27](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md#27-the-principles-of-the-law-of-events-and-what-follows-for-highlights-and-the-engine)); the Highlights line
  [decisions of 2026-09-19](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-19),
  "The law of events: no fields, no matter, no registers; only events".
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The law of events'
  engine, `events-v1`, was deleted the same day
  ([MIGRATION, "One engine, on 2026-09-19"](../../MIGRATION.md#one-engine-on-2026-09-19-the-old-engine-deleted));
  what remains of it is the vocabulary of the Beam Law: an event in
  transit is a row (`nature_beam.py:216`), a measured event is a body
  (`measured.py:477`, `Measured`), and the world file's `measured` and
  `families` keys ([ENGINE.md, the law of events](../../ENGINE.md#the-law-of-events-events-v1),
  the historical note).
- **(e) The reading.** None kept: every reading of `events-v1` was
  re-registered under the law of the ray the same day ([record 15](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md#15-decided-the-law-of-the-ray), "the
  series C and Bell readings are re-registered under it").
- **(f) Superseded.** The same day by the law of the ray (entry 6); the
  event-only vocabulary stands ("there is no ray, there is only an
  event", the owner, 2026-09-20, [record 21](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#21-and-there-is-no-ray-there-is-only-an-event)).

### 6. 2026-09-19: the law of the ray: a quantum is an integer row on the record; the collision a permutation; the click the only one-way border; an open face a detector

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A wave on the lattice (the
  coherent sum at a Node, the shares, the placement), a photon in flight,
  a scattering, a detector, the edge of the world.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "The Node holds no wave: no coherent
  sum, no shares, no placement, no tie order, no scatter; a unit is a ray
  with a record (its Node, an integer momentum vector, its age, its phase
  in Z_K stamped by its emitter's clock and turned by its family's
  declared steps per Link, its number, its amount, its content) and moves
  along the digital line of its momentum at one speed for every direction
  (at most one Link per interval; 1 / sqrt 3 in space ...), a bijection;
  when rays meet at a Node the seven-slot collision table (the 20 orbits
  of the 48 symmetries, amount and momentum conserved ...) permutes their
  directions, a bijection; a measured event measures by its table and a
  detector's click adds amplitudes, its record per interval the square of
  the coherent sum of what arrived (the wave is a reading of a crowd of
  rays and lives nowhere else); ... an open face is a detector; the click
  is the only one-way border" ([record 15](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md)). In the algebra's words: the
  state is a list of integer rows; the walk is a translation; the
  collision is a permutation of a finite set under the cube group of 48
  signed axis permutations; the click is the one non-invertible step.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** DECIDED, the model owner, 2026-09-19,
  after "Go with what we talked about, fast; there is no wave in the
  Node" ([record 14](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md#14-go-with-what-we-talked-about-fast-there-is-no-wave-in-the-node), [record 15](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md)); the Highlights line
  [decisions of 2026-09-19, "The law of the ray"](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-19);
  named the Beam Law on 2026-09-20 ([record 37](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#37-decided-the-law-of-the-ray-is-the-beam-law)); the migration entry
  [MIGRATION, "The law of the ray, on 2026-09-19"](../../MIGRATION.md#the-law-of-the-ray-on-2026-09-19-rays-v1).
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The row's record:
  `src/event_universe/events/nature_beam.py:216` (`NatureBeam`: Node,
  direction, age, phase, number, amount, content). The flight as a
  translation on the digital line: `:745` (`Flight`), `:803`
  (`walk_step`). The collision as a permutation generated from its
  classes under the 48: `:1203` (`collision_table`, "Generated from its
  rule over the classes, never written by hand"); the 48 themselves,
  `src/event_universe/core/game_board.py:37` (`cube_symmetries`), the
  hand as their pseudoscalar `:77`. The click as the one one-way step and
  the bijection of everything else: `src/event_universe/events/engine.py:570`
  (`step`) and `:628` (`inverse_step`). The face detectors:
  `src/event_universe/events/world.py:730` (`FACE_NAMES`) and
  `engine.py:1410` (`face_detectors`).
- **(e) The reading.** The Bell worlds under the law of the ray: S = 2
  exactly, every marginal 1/2 (DETECTOR, [record 71](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), NATURE row 1b); the
  isotropy of the flight, the cone test: both rows arrive at the same
  age on an axis and a diagonal, 17 and 24 Links at age 29 (DETECTOR,
  `examples/events/amplitude/expectations.json`, `cone`; [record 144](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#144-the-cone-test-of-the-paper-session-issue-376-approved-in-record-140-run-on-the-one-clicks-head-725d811f-no-src-change-the-flight-is-euclidean-to-one-intervals-rounding-in-every-direction-both-rows-arrive-at-age-29-on-the-axis-17-links-and-on-the-staircase-of-24-links-the-tables-1--sqrt-3-and-the-metric-of-the-phase-is-the-familys-declared-form-of-phase_per_link-the-integer-form-counts-the-links-stepped-51-and-8-mod-64-l1-and-the-pair-form-3-1-counts-the-intervals-of-age-23-and-23-euclidean-the-papers-row-1-the-octahedral-front-corrected-its-row-9-the-l1-path-difference-restricted-to-the-integer-form-so-the-two-slit-pearson-0368-of-slits_low-the-pair-form-is-not-explained-by-l1-the-isotropy-item-for-the-physicist-restated);
  NATURE row 5a, a BOUND on Q). The price named in the decision itself,
  "the clock beside a mass reads the presence of rays ... so it slows as
  M / r^2 and not M / r", was read later as series T's presence word,
  1.000 against 2.00 (DETECTOR, NATURE row 12, FAIL on the form), and
  resolved by the age word (entry 21).
- **(f) Superseded.** The identity `rays-v1` renamed `beam-v1` (the
  same law; [record 37](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 44](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#44-the-names-landed-naturebeam-gameboard-the-glossarys-single-names-the-beam-law)). The detector's record as the square
  of the coherent sum over a crowd (the reading `wave`) stands as a
  reading key; the click of a quantum is since 2026-09-20 the record's
  one click (entry 12), and its square is since 2026-09-21 the Gram form
  (entry 17). The seven-slot table became eight slots with the two rest
  slots (BEAM_LAW section 4).

### 7. 2026-09-19: the tables are generated from the keys; a detector's one reading is the moments of order 0, 1 and 2; the push is one bilinear form; charge is a rational per unit of content

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A detector's response to a field
  (density, current, stress); a force on a body; the charge and the mass
  of a particle.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "(1) the tables are generated from
  the keys and a world declares only what differs ..., `kind` derived
  from `quantum`; (3) the one reading function is the amount-weighted
  moments of order 0, 1 and 2 of the direction vectors, valid for fans as
  for the six headings ...; (2) the push as one bilinear form"
  ([record 18](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md#18-i-approve-1-and-3-and-2-with-the-physicist), "I approve 1 and 3, and 2 with the physicist"); "`charge`
  is the charge per unit of content of a family, an integer or a pair
  [n, d] ..., a measured event's charge is that times its content ...;
  the push is one product" ([record 26](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md#26-decided-charge-is-the-charge-per-unit-of-content-of-a-family-the-push-one-product)). So a reading is a scalar
  (order 0, the presence), a vector (order 1, the flow) and a tensor
  (order 2, traceless), a charge is a rational pair, and a force is a
  bilinear form.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-19
  ([record 18](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md), [record 26](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md)); the Highlights lines
  [decisions of 2026-09-19](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-19)
  ("I approve 1 and 3, and 2 with the physicist"; "`charge` is the
  charge per unit of content of a family").
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The one reading:
  `src/event_universe/events/nature_beam.py:522` (`read_arrivals`, "the
  moments of order 0, 1 and 2, exact integers"), `:365` (`Moments`:
  `presence`, `age`, `tensor`); the push as one signed inner product:
  `:2932` (`push_form`) through `src/event_universe/core/integer.py:155`
  (`signed_inner`, "a vector, a declared diagonal matrix of +1 and -1, a
  vector (the bilinear operation of the law)"); the charge as a pair:
  `src/event_universe/events/world.py:763` (`FamilyDefinition.charge`,
  "the pair (n, d)") and `core/integer.py:50` (`rational_sum`, a body's
  charge in a column); the tables from the keys: `world.py:855`
  (`default_rule`, "a free family (h = 0) is read ...; a paid one (h >=
  1) is measured").
- **(e) The reading.** The couplings' readings on the plane (series C,
  the rings and the probes' pushes) are GAMEBOARD diagnostics of worlds
  without a detector and no row pins them (the owner, 2026-09-22,
  [record ### 8. 2026-09-19: E = h f: a release costs the emitter by its phase rate; the free and the paid family one rule

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** Planck's relation E = h f (E
  the energy of a quantum, h Planck's constant, f the frequency); the
  photon's energy; the emitter's recoil.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "A release costs the emitter by its
  phase rate: a unit of a paid family costs `quantum` times the emitter's
  phase steps per self-creation (its content over K, the whole part off
  its clock), carries that as its momentum along its heading and gives it
  to what measures it, so that the energy of a click is proportional to
  the emitter's frequency, E = h f with h the declared `quantum`; a
  family without a phase circle turns by nothing and costs nothing, which
  is the free family, so the two kinds become one rule" ([record 10](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md#10-i-approve-the-proposal-a-release-costs-the-emitter-e--h-f-the-two-gaps)). In
  the algebra: E = h f is an integer identity, content = h x s with s the
  turn (an integer count of phase steps), and the kind of a family is the
  value of h (0 or not), never a branch.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** "I approve the proposal", the model
  owner, 2026-09-19 ([record 10](../../LOG_2026-09-19.md)); the Highlights line
  [decisions of 2026-09-19](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-19),
  "a release costs the emitter by its phase rate, E = h f".
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The quantum h on the
  family, `src/event_universe/events/world.py:763` (`FamilyDefinition.quantum`,
  "the content of one unit of it per phase step of its emitter's turn ...
  0 for a free family, 1 or more for a paid one: the kind is derived,
  never declared"); the release, `src/event_universe/events/nature_beam.py:5494`
  (`_release_family`); the engine's contract
  [ENGINE.md, "A release costs the emitter by its phase rate"](../../ENGINE.md#a-release-costs-the-emitter-by-its-phase-rate);
  the derivation, [DERIVATIONS_BEAM 6.4](../../DERIVATIONS_BEAM.md#64-e--h-f-from-the-release).
- **(e) The reading.** The paper's abstract states "Planck's and de
  Broglie's relations as identities under the declared dictionary"
  (`paper/general_formula/main.tex`, the abstract at line 43); the
  register's one set carries a flag on K, "the lamp's phase rate with its
  content, E = h f ..., set per world before any comparison and never
  after one" ([NATURE, the rules of the register](../../NATURE.md#the-rules-of-the-register),
  rule (a)); the click's energy is row 6 of the Inside | Outside table
  (entry 24).
- **(f) Superseded.** The turn read as "the whole part off its clock"
  (`by_clock`) became the accumulator's count under the fraction-free
  law on 2026-09-20 (entry 14; `by_clock` kept as the constant-rate
  identity, `core/integer.py:60`). E = h f itself was not replaced.

### 9. 2026-09-20: one mechanism for all the laws: a force of nature is a column with a sign and a lifetime; the coupling is a signed inner product over the columns

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** Gravity, the electric force,
  the strong force (its short range), the weak force (a decay), the
  binding of a nucleus.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "the one coupling as a signed inner
  product over the columns a family declares per unit of content, push =
  M_A x (sum over the columns c of epsilon_c x c_A x c_B) x flow ..., and
  gravity the column every family has with the value 1 and the sign
  minus, so that ... the strong force is a third column with the sign
  minus, not a term written in the code; a column's sign, plus for like
  values pushing apart and minus for like values pulling together, is a
  key and not a formula; (2) the lifetime L a key of any family ..., the
  range of every force; (3) the table entries generic: read, measure,
  rerelease, pass and the transformation, so the weak force is a
  short-lived family with the transformation entry" ([record 27](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#27-decided-one-mechanism-for-all-the-laws-on-the-gameboard)). So the
  coupling is the matrix **C** of the reader's charges per column, a
  declared table of bounded integer ratios with a sign per column; the
  strong force's range is an integer L on the row's age ([record 23](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#23-decided-the-strong-forces-range-is-a-lifetime-l), "the
  event whose age reaches L makes no next event but an escape click");
  the weak force is a transformation of families, `become`, with a
  window ([record 25](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#25-decided-the-weak-force-is-arranged-in-the-worlds-terms-and-the-law-is-asked-what-else-it-predicts), [record 78](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#78-landed-the-weak-force-weak-v1-in-the-next-pull-request)); the binding that costs content is a
  give at the contact ([record 115](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#115-decided-the-binding-that-costs-content-binding-v1-is-designed-by-the-physicist-read-only-after-the-one-click-lands-with-candidate-b-first-the-strong-familys-rows-paid-the-exchange-between-the-bound-bodies-as-content-in-flight-the-escape-click-at-l-as-the-released-binding-energy), [record 143](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#143-binding-v1-landed-pr-393-main-45c0eb48-the-physics-rule-review-mergeable-after-four-should-fix-none-blocking-the-rule-candidate-a-to-the-letter-and-every-pinned-integer-of-b1-b2-b3-reproduced-by-the-reviewers-own-replays-three-fixes-applied-before-the-merge-the-identity-and-given-from-the-run-time-give-the-designs-momentum-pin-outside-on-b1-by-270-720-on-x-exactly-6-x-3008-x-15-pushes-the-third-law-gap-of-record-126-made-visible-by-held-paid-content-that-counts-in-m_a-but-is-never-released-closed-once-both-gave-note-40s-readings-stated-b2s-amplitude-key-and-the-gate-replay-to-the-one-clicks-merge)).
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** "maybe one mechanism for all the laws
  on the board?"; "just check that it is correct and that it works", the
  model owner, 2026-09-20 ([record 27](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); the mathematician's verdict on
  the columns, correct and working, bit for bit on every registered
  integer ([record 35](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#35-the-mathematicians-verdict-on-the-columns-correct-and-working)); the Highlights line
  [decisions of 2026-09-20](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-20),
  "One mechanism for all the laws on the GameBoard". The first formula
  verified on paper before its build
  ([CODE_TO_FORMULAS, section 2](../../CODE_TO_FORMULAS.md#2-the-bridge-2026-09-20-day-formulas-checked-against-the-register)).
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The column and its sign:
  `src/event_universe/events/world.py:741` (`Column`), `:608`
  (`COLUMN_SIGNS = (1, -1)`), `:754` (`built_in_columns`: gravity first,
  charge second); the push as one signed inner product over the columns:
  `src/event_universe/events/nature_beam.py:2932` (`push_form`, "ONE
  signed inner product over the columns (the model owner's decision of
  2026-09-20, 'one mechanism for all the laws on the GameBoard')");
  the primitive, `src/event_universe/core/integer.py:155`
  (`signed_inner`); the lifetime on the family, `world.py:763`
  (`FamilyDefinition.lifetime`); the five table rules, `world.py:324`
  (`TABLES = ("read", "measure", "rerelease", "pass", "become")`); the
  contract, BEAM_LAW note 31 (`docs/BEAM_LAW.md:1863`).
- **(e) The reading.** The strong column and the lifetime: series I, the
  nucleus ([EXPERIMENTS, I](../../EXPERIMENTS.md#i-the-nucleus-2026-09-20));
  the binding: the deuteron's `bond` clicks on the border `lifetime`, the
  escaped content 4 of 3677 = 0.109 percent against nature's 0.1185
  percent (DETECTOR; NATURE row 7a, BOUND on the declared bond), the
  alpha's ratio 2.0 against 12.72 (NATURE row 7b, FAIL, "stated before
  the run as the law's failure"); the weak force: the shell's 64 beta
  clicks, the decay's width over its median 0.036 against the memoryless
  3.17 (DETECTOR; NATURE row 8a, FAIL). Gravity's and the electric
  column's detector readings are entry 7's D3 and X; the equivalence
  principle after a detector holds to the Node on every click (D3,
  DETECTOR). A FAIL is a result of the theory and stated as such
  ([NATURE, rule (e)](../../NATURE.md#the-rules-of-the-register)).
- **(f) Superseded.** The push's rational parts "taken by whole division
  off the clock" became, under the fraction-free law, counts on the
  reader's own accumulators per column and axis (entry 14, BEAM_LAW note
  41). The mechanism stands.

### 10. 2026-09-20: two kinds of readings; "our laws are on the GameBoard; in the detector one sees other laws"; the API is an emitter in and a detector out

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A measurement; the laws physics
  measures (Newton's, Coulomb's, Doppler's) against the law the engine
  runs.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** Two kinds of readings: "a detector
  reading, the only kind reality has, and a GameBoard reading, the host's
  view ('In reality there is no such thing'); a comparison with nature
  uses detector readings only" ([record 20](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#20-decided-two-kinds-of-readings-in-reality-there-is-no-such-thing)); "the GameBoard is not
  measurable by a human" ([record 22](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#22-decided-the-experimenters-skill-the-gameboard-is-not-measurable-by-a-human)); "Our laws are on the GameBoard; in
  the detector one sees other laws: a comparison with nature is always
  between detector readings and nature's laws, never between the
  GameBoard's rules and nature's laws" ([record 26](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#26-decided-our-laws-are-on-the-gameboard-in-the-detector-one-sees-other-laws)); "when you intervene
  in the GameBoard it requires a detector on the GameBoard or an emitter;
  that is the API" ([record 53](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#53-decided-the-api-of-the-gameboard-is-an-emitter-in-and-a-detector-out)); "In the end everything comes down to a
  transformation between the GameBoard and the world we experience"
  ([record 55](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#55-in-the-end-everything-comes-down-to-a-transformation-between-the-gameboard-and-the-world-we-experience)); "There is no outside" ([record 58](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#58-there-is-no-outside)). In the algebra: the
  Outside is the set of clicks and their counts; a law of physics is what
  a reading of the Inside looks like, never the Inside's rule itself.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-20 (the
  records above); the Highlights lines
  [decisions of 2026-09-20](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-20),
  "Two kinds of readings", "The experimenter's skill", "Our laws are on
  the GameBoard", "The API of the GameBoard".
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The readings by type,
  each labelled detector or GameBoard:
  [ENGINE.md, "The detector's readings by type"](../../ENGINE.md#the-detectors-readings-by-type);
  the detector set's record, `src/event_universe/events/measured.py:152`
  (`DetectorSet`); the run's `detectors` and `face_detectors` blocks,
  `src/event_universe/events/engine.py:1378` and `:1410`; the GameBoard
  diagnostics named so in code, `nature_beam.py:2218`
  (`GameBoardDiagnostics`).
- **(e) The reading.** The rule is the ground of every reading's label
  in NATURE and in the register; its sharpening: only a detector's
  reading is a measurement ([record 281](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#281-the-owners-rule-most-readings-are-and-must-be-measurements-after-a-detector-the-skills-and-the-project-say-so-at-the-top-since-many-confuse-a-number-measured-on-the-gameboard-with-a-measurement-which-is-always-after-a-detector-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0615z-translated-also-make-sure-that-of-course-most-of-the-readings-are-exactly-after-a-detector-we-do-this-in-the-skills-and-in-the-project-too-many-get-confused-and-take-results-they-measured-but-that-is-a-measurement-after-a-detector-and-that-is-what-causes-many-problems-because-it-is-only-possible-to-measure-through-a-detector-what-stood-already-the-experimenter-measures-only-behind-a-detector-or-at-an-external-thing-skillsexperimenter-the-simulation-runner-labels-every-registered-number-a-detector-reading-or-a-gameboard-reading-skillssimulation-runner-the-shared-workflows-point-6-the-observed-value-is-the-reading-and-never-the-boards-number-record-210-enginemds-readings-by-type-record-205-57s-nobody-sees-the-state-the-54-line-of-record-277-added-now-so-that-every-role-reads-it-first-1-agentsmd-change-boundaries-one-bullet-a-measurement-is-a-detectors-reading-a-click-a-records-moments-an-external-things-reading-as-declared-in-the-world-file-only-a-measurement-is-compared-with-nature-or-pinned-as-an-expectation-a-gameboard-reading-the-hosts-view-of-stores-books-presences-and-phases-is-a-diagnostic-and-is-labelled-so-wherever-it-appears-a-number-whose-kind-is-not-named-is-not-a-result-2-skillsworkflowmd-under-record-205s-refinement-point-7-with-the-owners-words-and-the-same-rule-for-every-role-3-one-54-line-no-rule-of-the-law-changes-the-registers-labels-stand-record-163-5-the-pins-as-detector-readings-the-papers-confrontation-table-cites-only-detector-readings-and-says-so-record-275-2)), no experiment reads the
  GameBoard and the interference wall is a detector ([record 562](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#562-the-owners-word-no-experiment-reads-the-gameboard-only-after-a-detector-every-external-entity-is-a-detector-or-an-emitter-the-interference-wall-is-an-external-entity-the-audit-ordered-2026-09-22-about-0001z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-0002z-the-owner-translated-make-sure-no-experiment-reads-from-the-board-only-after-a-detector-all-external-entities-are-either-a-detector-or-an-emitter-a-board-reading-is-for-diagnostics-and-bug-fixing-alone-the-interference-wall-is-an-external-entity-make-sure-the-highlight-writes-this-the-decision-one-highlights-line-under-the-decisions-of-2026-09-21-the-owners-word-of-2026-09-22-extending-record-281s-line-every-experiment-reads-only-after-a-detector-every-external-entity-of-a-world-is-a-detector-or-an-emitter-a-gameboard-reading-serves-diagnostics-and-bug-fixing-alone-and-is-never-pinned-compared-with-nature-or-a-passfails-ground-the-interference-wall-the-screen-the-pattern-falls-on-is-an-external-entity-a-detector-the-tree-today-the-two-slit-world-exampleseventstwo_slitsjson-declares-the-screen-as-detectors-screen_0-to-screen_n-one-position-each-threshold-1-reading-wave-read-by-their-clicks-and-the-slit-wall-as-the-entity-wall_material-the-audit-of-the-rest-is-ordered-ordered-the-physics-rule-reviewer-session_01nzjtpwvmsllfpwjln1bfog-read-only-about-one-hour-on-main-at-060501c0-or-later-a-every-pinned-nature-compared-or-passfail-deciding-number-of-the-register-by-its-source-detector-or-gameboard-b-every-tools_readingspy-by-what-it-prints-from-the-events-and-from-statejson-and-whether-a-passfail-uses-a-gameboard-number-c-every-external-entity-of-every-world-file-a-detector-or-an-emitter-the-two-slit-screen-named-d-every-gameboard-number-that-is-pinned-compared-or-decides-is-a-finding-with-the-smallest-fix-enginemds-table-the-detectors-readings-by-type-the-reference-a-wrong-row-of-it-a-finding-the-fixes-then-to-the-replicator-labels-and-pins-or-the-architect-a-world-file-as-bounded-orders)), a

### 11. 2026-09-20: the masses and the charges are the initialisation; the law quantises what lives on a compact group and leaves free what lives on a scale

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The mass of a particle, the
  mass ratios, the charges; why nature gives a number for the phase and
  the charge but not for the masses.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** A mass is a content, an integer M
  on Z, the non-compact scale; the phase, the direction and the charge
  live on compact groups (Z_N, the sphere's fan, the rationals per unit
  of content) and are quantised by the widths N, P, Q, W, G, K, "the
  sizes of finite samplings of compact manifolds"; "every map of the law
  linear or homogeneous in them, so no number of the law selects a mass"
  ([record 179](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#179-the-owners-word-on-the-mass-numbers-2026-09-21-0044z-through-the-mathematician-the-owners-question-why-does-nature-give-a-number-for-n-and-the-quantum-things-but-not-for-the-masses-what-is-the-vector-reason-for-the-mass-number-if-it-has-one-and-his-word-pass-to-the-boss-write-the-sentence-in-predictions-and-let-it-be-written-in-highlights-too-predictions-entry-26-the-law-quantises-exactly-what-lives-on-a-compact-group-and-leaves-free-what-lives-on-a-scale-charge-phase-and-direction-have-numbers-mass-has-none-the-widths-n-p-q-w-g-k-the-sizes-of-finite-samplings-of-compact-manifolds-every-observable-on-one-quantised-by-them-the-amounts-on-the-non-compact-scale-every-map-of-the-law-linear-or-homogeneous-in-them-so-no-number-of-the-law-selects-a-mass-a-mass-ratio-only-from-a-nonlinear-closure-on-amounts-which-the-law-does-not-have-by-record-106-the-same-division-as-natures-a-structural-agreement-not-fitted-the-54-line-in-the-owners-words-as-given-pr-413), PREDICTIONS 26); "the masses and charges are the
  initialisation, the catalog of the families, not derivable in this law"
  ([record 106](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#106-decided-the-masses-and-charges-are-the-initialisation-the-catalog-of-the-families-not-derivable-in-this-law-the-two-sentences-superseded-the-binding-design-pending)); the minimal mass is one unit, a theorem of the law

### 12. 2026-09-20: the amplitude law: a quantum is a record; the GameBoard computes every future and the click chooses one by the wheel; the world is the list of clicks; the wave is the histogram of the clicks

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The wave function of one
  quantum, its collapse at a measurement, the probability of an outcome,
  entanglement, the interference of one quantum with itself.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** A record: every row of one birth
  carries the birth's identity, its branch (a label) and its multiplicity
  (an integer, the product of the norms of the splits it passed); "the
  GameBoard computes every branch, locally and exactly ...; absorption at
  a detector is an offer ...; the world is a reading: for each record its
  offers in order, the cumulative, and u, the birth phase on the
  GameBoard, chooses one; the choice is the transformation between the
  GameBoard and the world we experience, not a process on the GameBoard"
  ([record 72](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#72-decided-locality-must-not-be-harmed-the-gameboard-is-the-future-that-could-be-the-click-chooses-one), the owner: "Locality must not be harmed. The GameBoard is
  the future that could be; the click chooses one"); "the click is the
  one operation that changes the possibilities: it removes combinations
  from a record ... and adds one row to the world's list; everything else
  on the GameBoard is invertible" ([record 80](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#80-the-click-defined-by-what-it-does)); "The world is the list of
  clicks. ... Classical and quantum are the same GameBoard at a different
  density of clicks. One law, one measurement, one world. ... in the
  world the histogram of the clicks is the wave" ([record 77](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#77-the-corrected-sentence-without-a-caveat-and-the-wave)). In the
  algebra: the click is a threshold comparison of the record's coordinate
  u (a scalar on the wheel Z_W) against the rungs of the cells'
  cumulative weights, `2 T u + T <= 2 W C_k`, with no division; the
  probability is a frequency over records.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-20: the
  amplitude option 1 ([record 63](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#63-decided-the-amplitude-option-1-now)), then "Locality must not be harmed"
  ([record 72](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)), the click by what it does ([record 80](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)), the corrected
  sentence ([record 77](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)), "go for it with the four recommendations and
  the unifications" ([record 86](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#86-decided-amplitude-v1-is-built-with-the-four-recommendations-and-the-four-unifications)); the one click landed, every lamp a
  record, the key `amplitude` deleted ([record 145](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#145-the-one-click-landed-pr-395-main-e4b649a0-stage-vii-step-4-of-amplitude-v1-under-record-137-1-and-2-every-lamp-births-records-one-click-per-record-by-the-ladder-u-the-records-own-field-the-push-by-share-the-key-amplitude-deleted-as-built-the-gate-reviews-three-items-closed-b-the-layer-releases-a-gathered-records-offers-bit-identical-on-14-of-14-completed-gate-worlds-and-the-four-k-worlds-w27_beams-memory-bounded-at-33-gb-but-its-time-open-c-a1s-verdict-from-the-gathers-the-ladder-lands-five-u-alone-on-the-screen-no-record-visibility-formed-the-gate-set-of-sixteen-all-and-only-the-lamp-worlds-changed-b2-re-run-under-the-one-click-with-its-binding-pins-unchanged-the-fraction-free-unification-started-from-its-head-the-architects-pr-3-and-phase-2-signalled), PR #395); the
  Highlights lines [decisions of 2026-09-20](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-20)
  ("Locality must not be harmed", "The corrected sentence", "The click
  defined by what it does", "`amplitude-v1` is built"); the design,
  `docs/designs/amplitude-v1/DESIGN.md`; the contract, BEAM_LAW note 37
  (`docs/BEAM_LAW.md:2623`); the migration,
  [MIGRATION, (vii-4) the one click](../../MIGRATION.md#the-amplitude-law-on-2026-09-20-vii-4-the-one-click-the-record-form-the-law).
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The record's three
  columns and the birth phase on the row,
  `src/event_universe/events/nature_beam.py:216` (`NatureBeam.record`,
  `branch`, `multiplicity`, `birth`); the layer and the ladder,
  `src/event_universe/events/amplitude.py:434` (`Layer`), `:241`
  (`rungs`, "b_k = (2 W C_k + Total) // (2 Total)"), `:275` (`cell_of`,
  "the comparison of two products ... no division and no rounding"),
  `:901` (`complete`); the gather, `nature_beam.py:6284`
  (`gather_records`, "one click per record"); the merge's cancel of an
  antiphase pair, `:6215` (`_merge`).
- **(e) The reading.** Bell's S on the record's pair, 176/64 = 2.75 at
  N = 64, E = 44, -44, 44, 44 in the unit 64, every marginal 32/64
  (DETECTOR; NATURE row 1a, PASS at 1.65 standard errors of Hensen et al.
  2015 and 0.078 below the quantum bound; at N = 1024 and 4096 the
  register reads 2.828125, row 1a's note); the Mach-Zehnder clicks 64/0
  over 64 births (DETECTOR; NATURE row 2b, PASS); the two-slit clicks'
  visibility 0.966 against 0.98 with the cause named, the fan's grain
  (DETECTOR; NATURE row 2a, FAIL); Malus's law exact at 45 and 90
  degrees (DETECTOR; NATURE row 9, PASS). The paper's one prediction, S
  = 181/64 at the powers of two from 512, is this reading's closed form
  (the abstract, `paper/general_formula/main.tex:43`; [record 102](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#102-the-paper-sessions-checks-issue-368-sn-is-not-monotone-and-not-bounded-by-4n-records-89-and-93-superseded-on-those-points-the-tie-the-tables-norm-the-rotation-the-windowed-which-path-the-phase-bound)).
- **(f) Superseded.** The threshold on the pointer's square ([record 40](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#40-decided-issue-359-step-a-yes-step-b-no),
  step A) by the amount threshold at the one click ([record 135](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#135-stage-vii-step-4-built-on-the-branch-the-one-click-the-record-form-the-law-every-lamp-births-records-the-key-amplitude-deleted-the-crowd-threshold-deleted-the-8-lamp-free-gate-worlds-byte-identical-to-main-the-7-lamp-worlds-changed-by-the-record-form-itself-the-stop-rule-read-as-the-crowd-threshold-series--every-lamp-world-the-k-table-unchanged-the-share-worlds-integers-as-expected-w27_beams-host-cost-7-gb-not-completed-the-gate-review-running),
  [record 145](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); the ladder normalised at the nearest integer by the
  fraction-free comparison of products ([record 155](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md) (3), `cell_of`); u
  as the count of births mod N by the wheel at a declared rate (entry
  18); the pointer's square by the Gram form (entry 17). The record, the
  offer, the ladder and the one click per record stand.

### 13. 2026-09-20: the record's push by share, the step drive, and the fraction-free law: every count is an accumulator on the reader's own record

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A body's velocity and its
  acceleration under a force; the rate of a clock; the count a clock owes
  in a potential; the rate of an emitter; every quantity physics writes
  as a fraction or a real number.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "the count of Links as the whole
  part of the driven distance": "the step counts the whole part of the
  distance the momentum has driven, one bounded integer per axis on the
  body's own record, bit-identical at a constant momentum, never two
  Links in one interval; a generic count primitive if it fits the hour"
  ([record 108](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#108-decided-note-17s-no-remainder-is-kept-superseded-for-the-moving-body-the-count-of-the-driven-distance-on-the-bodys-own-record-a-generic-form-if-it-can-be), the owner: "yes; let him give a generic solution if he
  can"); then the fraction-free law, "every count an accumulator on the
  reader's record, the drive's rule made the rule of every count"
  ([record 133](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#133-the-fraction-free-law-the-mathematician-read-only-every-count-an-accumulator-on-the-readers-record-the-drives-rule-made-the-rule-of-every-count-constant-rate-counts-bit-identical-proved-the-push-and-the-doppler-weight-exact-over-a-period-and-within-one-unit-per-interval-the-48-covariance-kept-the-floors-never-broke-it-the-ties-do-one-unification-after-todays-four-merges-recommended-the-pushed-worlds-re-registered-once-the-owners-decision-pending), the mathematician's form; [record 148](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#148-the-physicists-reading-of-the-varying-rate-count-read-only-scratchpad-fraction_free_reviewreviewmd-at-most-200-intervals-per-run-the-accumulator-is-the-laws-count-under-e--h-f-the-turns-of-a-clock-whose-rate-changes-are-the-whole-part-of-the-integral-of-its-rate-by_clock-re-prices-the-whole-age-at-todays-rate-and-drifts-without-bound-1-in-160-9-in-20-000-1089-in-200-000-births-on-the-a0_b0-lamp-note-17s-no-remainder-exemption-holds-only-at-a-constant-rate-which-no-registered-lamp-or-crowd-has-the-bell-failures-are-the-tools-and-tests-tick-windows-not-the-correlations-read-by-birth-ordinal-s--17664-exactly-on-both-trees-mz_equal-64-the-deuterons-69-waits-are-the-laws-count-and-bound-stands-with-re-pinned-ticks-recommend-i-decided-by-the-boss-under-the-standing-order-reversing-record-147s-narrowing-the-full-unification-built-the-bell-readers-re-aligned-by-record-the-register-re-registered-once-in-one-batch-note-17-corrected-note-41-the-laws-one-count)): in the owner's
  conversation with the Boss, a rate 3/10 is "acc += 3; if acc >= 10:
  acc -= 10, a step ... the body's counts a point on the integer torus
  Z^n / d Z^n, translated by the rate vector r = C a, every wall crossing
  an event" ([record 155](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). This is the sixth verb: the Euclidean
  division with the remainder kept, whose carry is the event.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-20
  ([record 108](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md); "what does fraction-free give?", [record 150](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#150-the-owner-back-at-2210z-on-the-fraction-free-law-what-does-fraction-free-give-the-owners-words-translated-does-this-concern-the-slowing-or-one-global-tick-for-all-with-a-slowing-do-you-think-nature-works-this-way-how-can-it-be-fixed-in-one-place-with-a-generic-rule-where-is-the-table-the-bosss-answers-one-primitive-for-every-count-the-exact-count-of-a-varying-rate-the-global-tick-untouched-and-the-slowing-local-only-its-arithmetic-exact-the-accumulator-the-form-of-every-physical-clock-the-integral-of-its-rate-the-residue-the-phase-within-the-cycle-the-caveat-that-nd-in-bounded-integers-is-the-models-hypothesis-directed-the-counts-as-one-table-on-the-bodys-record-one-row-per-count-and-one-loop-at-the-self-creation-ordered-to-the-implementer-as-the-last-step-of-stage-e); DECIDED
  23:30Z, "go for it: no registers at Nodes, no tables", [record 155](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md));

### 14. 2026-09-20 to 21: Doppler emerges by itself: the key `doppler` and its grain G deleted; a moving reader's Doppler is the count of the rows it crosses

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The Doppler shift of a moving
  receiver and of a moving source, 1 + v/c and 1 - v/c.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** A counting identity and no rule: "a
  row and a body meet ONCE at the crossing of their world lines"
  ([record 158](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#158-the-crossing-rule-designed-record-153s-order-the-physicist-read-only-scratchpad-crossingdesignmd-with-crossing_simpy-main-unchanged-a-row-and-a-body-meet-once-at-the-crossing-of-their-world-lines-a-body-at-rest-reads-arrivals-as-today-in-the-interval-of-a-step-it-also-meets-the-rows-that-crossed-its-own-link-the-other-way-and-the-rows-resident-at-the-destination-moving-against-it-and-it-does-not-meet-the-rows-moving-with-it-the-rows-behind-it-on-its-link-or-one-interval-later-the-rows-it-met-at-the-origin-the-leapfrog-the-bodys-own-two-last-links-are-the-only-facts-nothing-at-a-node-proved-on-the-experimenters-streams-toward-k--4-45-reads-in-32-intervals-k--8-58-in-48-away-19-in-32-38-in-48-rest-48-no-row-twice-none-missed-the-integer-form-moves-_move-before-the-law-and-makes-met-the-crossing-expression-doppler-v1-and-g-deleted-with-the-list-the-emergent-doppler-exact-on-the-axis-and-the-transverse-an-encounter-count-on-a-fan-direction-a-limit-to-state-not-to-tune-admissible)); "no Doppler rule is added and none is needed; the key
  `doppler`, its weight and the grain G are deleted with the crossing
  rule, after which the reader's Doppler is the count of the rows it
  meets, 1 + v/c and 1 - v/c exactly on an axis" ([record 163](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#163-decided-the-owner-2355z-on-the-bosss-list-of-six-1-yes-the-fan-as-a-width-p-of-the-law-with-the-angle-weights-as-the-rule-of-every-fan-huygens-on-the-lattice-record-160-2-yes-the-exact-phase-at-the-click-from-the-rows-two-accumulators-record-156-5-yes-the-registers-pins-as-detector-readings-only-6-rule-a-closed-pr-383-record-139-3-the-birth-wheel-and-4-the-fan-direction-doppler-left-with-two-questions-explain-why-doppler-is-needed-when-it-emerges-by-itself-what-is-the-birth-wheel-the-owners-words-translated-the-bosss-answers-nothing-is-added-for-doppler-the-key-and-g-are-deleted-the-crossing-rule-alone-gives-it-the-lattices-own-count-on-a-fan-direction-is-the-laws-statement-so-4-is-closed-by-the-question-itself-and-the-wheel-is-the-lamps-count-written-on-the-record-at-birth-that-the-ladder-reads-at-the-click-in-place-of-a-draw), the
  owner: "explain why Doppler is needed when it emerges by itself?"). The
  step reads the crossed Link: the two last Links of the body are the
  only facts.
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-20, "go" for
  the one-world test with the key off ([record 151](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#151-the-owner-2240z-to-2300z-why-do-we-need-to-remember-anything-when-at-the-node-only-a-click-knows-how-to-count-the-world-does-not-see-what-happens-on-the-board-the-boss-the-accumulator-is-the-pre-click-state-of-the-clock-not-a-memory-of-the-board-the-registers-evidence-is-detector-readings-the-internal-counts-diagnostics-the-digests-a-host-tool-proposed-the-register-pins-detector-readings-only-why-is-there-a-doppler-does-it-not-happen-by-itself-from-the-laws-the-boss-the-emission-doppler-is-automatic-from-the-flight-the-readers-doppler-was-built-as-the-key-doppler-because-a-body-reads-at-rest-between-its-steps-if-the-step-reads-the-row-on-the-link-it-crosses-the-readers-doppler-is-1--v-by-the-law-alone-and-the-key-is-redundant-if-not-the-hole-is-in-the-step-and-is-fixed-there-in-both-cases-the-key-leaves-the-code-and-then-we-get-rid-of-the-doppler-in-the-code-yes-in-both-cases-decided-go-for-the-one-world-test-a-reader-stepping-one-link-per-k-intervals-toward-and-away-from-a-lamp-at-rest-the-key-off-k--4-and-8-the-expectation-k--1-and-k---1-if-the-step-reads-the-crossed-link-k-and-k-otherwise)); the test read
  ([record 153](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#153-the-readers-doppler-test-record-151-the-owners-go-main-0b45aa53-no-src-change-the-step-does-not-read-the-crossed-link-a-reader-stepping-one-link-per-k-intervals-reads-k-rows-per-k-intervals-toward-the-lamp-and-k-away-the-rate-at-rest-k--4-4-4-4-4-4-4-4-4-k--8-8-x-6-both-senses-so-the-readers-doppler-does-not-emerge-from-the-law-as-built-the-cause-the-node-reads-arrivals-only-and-the-step-comes-after-the-reading-so-toward-the-lamp-the-rows-resident-on-the-destination-when-the-reader-enters-are-never-read-172-per-step-the-doppler-excess-and-away-the-rows-read-at-the-origin-catch-up-and-are-read-again-172-per-step-the-deficit-refilled-the-key-doppler-supplies-exactly-the-missed-factor-1--vc--143-at-k--4-as-a-weight-572-rows-equivalent-per-4-intervals--k--172-not-as-a-count-per-the-owners-decision-the-key-leaves-the-code-and-the-hole-is-fixed-in-the-step-once-and-generic-the-crossing-rule-a-row-and-a-body-meet-once-at-their-crossing-never-twice-the-physicists-design-ordered)); the rule designed ([record 158](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); DECIDED 23:55Z

- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The step before the law

### 16. 2026-09-21, 00:08Z to 02:35Z: the whole law is one vector operation on the integer torus; the six verbs; the main course; the three tests

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The dynamics as a whole: the
  motion of light and of bodies, the fields, the clocks, the measurement;
  the distinction between classical and quantum physics.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "the whole law is one vector
  operation on the integer torus (s <- s + r; e <- [s >= d]; s <- s - e
  d, at every Node every interval ...), one piecewise-linear map, the
  owner's 'infinite activity'; the distinction that matters is in the
  rate: constant ... gives the closed form floor(s_0 + r t), the quantum
  part between clicks, computed at once ...; state-dependent (r = C a
  ...) gives s_{t+1} = F(s_t) with no closed form, solved by the
  iteration or by the continuum limit ds/dt = r(s), where Newton, Coulomb,
  Doppler and Bohr appear or fail to; the classical-quantum boundary one
  question, does a row's rate depend on what it meets; the click the one
  threshold that is read out" ([record 167](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#167-the-owners-statement-through-the-paper-session-0008z-2026-09-21-asked-for-and-accepted-by-the-owner-pass-this-to-the-boss-and-to-the-derivation-mathematician-the-whole-law-is-one-vector-operation-on-the-integer-torus-s---s--r-e---s--d-s---s---e-d-at-every-node-every-interval-the-flight-the-phase-every-count-the-ladder-and-the-clicks-evaluation-its-components-one-piecewise-linear-map-the-owners-infinite-activity-the-distinction-that-matters-is-in-the-rate-constant-the-phase-per-interval-a-directions-speed-gives-the-closed-form-floors_0--r-t-the-quantum-part-between-clicks-computed-at-once-bell-mach-zehnder-the-cone-the-two-slits-map-state-dependent-r--c-a-with-the-arrivals-depending-on-the-positions-the-push-the-collision-the-crowd-a-windowed-detector-gives-s_t1--fs_t-with-no-closed-form-solved-by-the-iteration-or-by-the-continuum-limit-dsdt--rs-where-newton-coulomb-doppler-and-bohr-appear-or-fail-to-the-classical-quantum-boundary-one-question-does-a-rows-rate-depend-on-what-it-meets-the-click-the-one-threshold-that-is-read-out-the-derivations-five-items-a-to-e-routed-to-the-derivation-mathematician-as-the-form-of-its-document)). Then the six verbs: "the
  system is integer vectors and tensors with operations on them ...: the
  state integer vectors on tori and the reading's order-2 tensor; the law
  one map of six operations per interval at every Node" ([record 181](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)):
  the translation of an accumulator by its rate, the bilinear form with a
  declared matrix, the group-ring addition, the permutation, the
  evaluation, the Euclidean division with the remainder kept
  ([HIGHLIGHTS 5.7, "The six operations"](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#57-from-the-world-we-see-to-the-vector-world-the-conversions-the-principles-what-is-derived-and-what-is-input-2026-09-21)).
  The admission test: generic, vector, local ([record 202](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). The order
  of work: equations first, runs as confirmation ([record 176](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#176-decided-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0100z-through-the-paper-session-the-owners-words-translated-add-it-to-skills-ask-the-boss-to-add-it-to-their-skills-and-this-is-the-main-course-we-need-to-take-the-main-course-equations-first-and-runs-as-confirmation-the-law-one-piecewise-linear-map-on-the-integer-torus-where-the-rate-is-constant-rows-between-clicks-the-map-has-the-closed-form-floors_0--r-t-and-every-number-is-computed-from-the-formula-before-any-run-a-run-a-confirmation-and-never-the-source-of-a-number-where-the-rate-depends-on-the-state-the-map-is-a-difference-equation-whose-solutions-are-the-iteration-the-engine-and-the-continuum-limit-where-newton-coulomb-doppler-bohr-appear-or-fail-to-every-derived-formula-meets-known-physics-twice-in-the-limit-and-at-finite-resolution-against-a-registered-integer-the-classical-quantum-boundary-the-property-of-the-rate-measured-as-rows-per-record-and-clicks-per-record-written-into-skillsworkflowmd-as-a-section-read-by-every-role-with-one-line-in-the-boss-mathematical-physics-and-experimenter-skills)).
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-21: the
  statement through the paper session, accepted, "pass this to the Boss
  and to the derivation mathematician" ([record 167](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); the derivation

  (the introduction's first paragraph, the equation `eq:map`).

### 17. 2026-09-21, 00:50Z to 01:40Z: the click without amplitudes: the record is an element of the group ring Z[Z_N]; the weight is one bilinear form f^T G f; Born's rule is the unique positive quadratic form; the complex numbers leave the code

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The probability amplitude as a
  complex number, the Born rule's square, the interference of two arms,
  Bell's and GHZ's products of amplitudes.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "the record is the phase-count
  vector **f** in Z^N; the amplitude (X, Y) = **E f** with **E** the 2 x
  N matrix of the rounded cosine and sine rows; the weight X^2 + Y^2 =
  **f**^T **G f** with **G** = **E**^T **E** the Gram matrix, one bilinear
  form on the record, bit-identical by the associativity of integer
  arithmetic; the arms' products (Bell, GHZ) the multiplication in the
  group ring Z[Z_N], a convolution ...; so the click is convolutions and
  additions in the ring, one bilinear form and one threshold, and the
  complex numbers (Z[i], `cmul`) leave the code" ([record 188](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#188-decided-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0140z-the-owners-words-translated-yes-if-it-cleans-a-non-vector-operation-off-the-gameboard-the-click-without-amplitudes-on-the-bosss-answer-to-the-owners-question-is-there-a-vector-operation-that-replaces-the-amplitudes-the-record-is-the-phase-count-vector-f-in-zn-the-amplitude-x-y--e-f-with-e-the-2-x-n-matrix-of-the-rounded-cosine-and-sine-rows-the-weight-x2--y2--ft-g-f-with-g--et-e-the-gram-matrix-one-bilinear-form-on-the-record-bit-identical-by-the-associativity-of-integer-arithmetic-the-arms-products-bell-ghz-the-multiplication-in-the-group-ring-zz_n-a-convolution-and-the-label-rotation-the-same-in-zz_2n-the-coherent-sum-within-a-node-tuple-and-the-incoherent-sum-across-tuples-one-block-diagonal-g-so-the-click-is-convolutions-and-additions-in-the-ring-one-bilinear-form-and-one-threshold-and-the-complex-numbers-zi-cmul-leave-the-code-the-form-is-the-lattice-gleasons-at-c_1--1-record-185-the-host-cost-n2-per-cell-for-2-n-the-owners-yes-conditional-on-the-change-removing-a-non-vector-operation-from-the-gameboard-which-it-does-the-evaluation-into-zi-and-the-complex-product-are-replaced-by-the-rings-convolution-and-the-one-bilinear-form-built-as-item-1b-of-the-click-pull-request-by-the-click-implementer-bit-identical-no-re-registration-the-derivation-mathematicians-67-ordered-before-the-decision-proves-the-identity-and-checks-it-integer-by-integer-on-mz_equal-slits_low-and-the-bell-cells-the-owners-question-of-the-same-message-what-is-the-input-of-the-whole-story-the-mass-n-and-g-and-something-else-what-is-our-input-to-our-physical-world-and-what-is-the-input-to-the-real-world-answered-in-record-189)). Before
  it, the owner's order: "first solve the matter of the amplitudes: how
  not to do a square in the code but a vector operation" ([record 173](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#173-the-owners-order-2026-09-21-0050z-the-owners-words-translated-first-solve-the-matter-of-the-amplitudes-how-not-to-do-a-square-in-the-code-but-a-vector-operation-the-bosss-form-the-clicks-weight-is-the-inner-product-of-the-pointer-with-itself-w--z-z-the-same-primitive-as-the-couplings-signed-inner-product-over-the-columns-a-vector-a-declared-matrix-a-vector-and-the-pointer-z-is-the-linear-image-of-the-records-phase-histogram-f-under-the-declared-2-x-2-matrices-of-the-splits-and-rotations-so-w--ft-g-f-with-g-the-circulant-gram-matrix-of-the-tables-g_pq--c_p-c_q--s_p-s_q-the-records-second-moment-against-the-detectors-matrix-the-same-class-as-the-readings-order-2-tensor-nothing-squared-as-a-step-of-its-own-bit-identical-ordered-as-the-first-item-of-the-click-pull-request-started-now-from-main-by-a-second-implementer)),

[record 328](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)).
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** The Gram matrix built
  once per N from the tables, `src/event_universe/core/phase.py:61`
  (`phase_gram`, "G_jk = C_j C_k + S_j S_k over those rounded entries
  themselves, never the cosine of j - k, so that f^T G f equals |E f|^2
  for every integer vector f"); the form, `src/event_universe/events/amplitude.py:788`
  (`gram_form`, "no pointer is formed"), `:779` (`gram_entry`), the
  evaluation `:766` (`evaluate`, a report), the ring's product `:158`
  (`ring_product`, "the cyclic convolution over the circle of N steps"),
  the cells `:817` (`cells`, "nothing squared as a step of its own"), all
  through `core/integer.py:155` (`signed_inner`). Across several arms the
  built click multiplies the arms' pointers in Z[i] (`amplitude.py:134`,
  `cmul`) and takes the sum with itself through `signed_inner`, the same
  form on the tensor product through its factorisation (the docstrings
  of `ring_product` and `cells`; 6.7's correction of the several-arm
  form).
- **(e) The reading.** Bit-identical: `mz_equal`'s offers 1681/1682 and
  1/1682 at every u and its clicks 64/0; `slits_low`'s 126 sets, the
  same integer both ways on every (set, Node, u), the registered 64
  clicks returned (6.7, a host computation on the engine's tables);
  the register's readings unchanged at the landing ([record 324](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). The
  power of the form: NATURE row 2c, the window [1.917, 2.012) from the
  click cells, NOT COMPARED to Sinha et al. 2010, "a read-back of an
  engine with the square built in, an implementation gate on the click's
  form and not evidence about nature" (the owner, 2026-09-22). In the

### 18. 2026-09-21, 01:05Z to 01:50Z: the vector program from group theory: the lattice as the translation group, the 48 with the hand, the wheel as a count on Z_W, c as the norm of the flight operator, rows and bodies

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The space (a lattice), its
  symmetries (rotations and reflections), the random draw of quantum
  mechanics, the speed of light c as a measured constant, light and
  matter as two kinds.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "the representation throughout: the
  state a vector on a product of tori; the lattice the translation group
  of the GameBoard acting by shift operators; the record an element of
  the group ring Z[Z_N]; the click the ring's convolution and the Gram
  form **G**; the collision a permutation; the hand a pseudoscalar of the
  cube's group of 48; the fan the angular measure on the sphere's
  directions; the six verbs the only operations" ([record ### 19. 2026-09-21, 02:05Z to 03:05Z: the world through a detector in vectors and tensors; the readings by type; a formula gives, a run proves; the transformation from the GameBoard to the observed values, the dictionary

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A distance, a time, a speed, a
  mass, an energy, a force, an angle, a probability, and c: every value
  physics measures.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "the world is the state vector **s**
  on the product of tori, evolved by the map **F** ...; nobody sees **s**;
  a detector reads its Node's neighbourhood by one linear reading **R**:
  the moments of the arriving rows, order 0 a scalar (the presence),
  order 1 a vector (the flow, the pointer), order 2 a tensor (the spread,
  traceless), and the age moment ...: the same content as physics'
  stress-energy tensor **T** (density, current, stress), so a detector
  reads **T**, never a metric; the click is the Gram form **G** on the
  record vector **f** = **R s** and one threshold [u < b_k]: the world
  becomes a bit; ... every known law is the composition of a reading with
  the evolution in a limit" ([record 196](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). "every value we see in the
  world is a detector's reading, so a distance, a time, a speed, a mass,
  an energy, a force, an angle or a probability is produced from the
  GameBoard's Links, intervals, contents, phase steps and weights only
  through a named reading; a GameBoard quantity is never compared with
  nature directly" ([record 210](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#210-decided-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0255z-the-owners-words-translated-so-there-is-a-good-direction-for-the-total-computation-to-reduce-a-degree-of-freedom-note-that-the-values-we-see-in-the-world-are-behind-a-detector-including-distance-speed-and-the-like-they-must-undergo-a-transformation-to-how-it-looks-on-the-gameboard-if-you-agree-put-it-in-the-highlights-two-things-1-the-direction-of-record-209-approved-the-one-constant-k-per-node-per-interval-as-the-candidate-that-reduces-the-grains-degrees-of-freedom-section-13-ordered-2-the-transformation-from-the-gameboard-to-the-observed-values-agreed-by-the-boss-and-written-into-highlights-57-with-the-first-dictionary-every-value-we-see-in-the-world-is-a-detectors-reading-so-a-quantity-of-the-gameboard-links-intervals-content-units-phase-steps-the-fans-directions-the-gram-weights-becomes-an-observed-value-a-distance-a-time-a-speed-a-mass-an-energy-a-force-an-angle-a-probability-only-through-a-named-reading-the-derivations-targets-being-those-transformations-a-distance-is-a-rows-round-trip-flight-time-or-the-fans-angle-never-the-link-count-a-time-is-a-bodys-own-turn-count-its-proper-time-and-its-owed-count-a-speed-is-what-a-moving-reader-counts-doppler-1---v--c-the-crossing-rule-or-a-flight-time-between-two-detectors-a-mass-is-the-push-a-body-reads-the-clocks-slowing-near-it-and-the-mass-a-detector-reads-of-a-bound-body-records-79-115-an-energy-or-frequency-is-the-cost-per-phase-step-e--h-f-and-the-clicks-amount-a-force-is-the-momentum-change-a-bodys-drive-shows-an-angle-is-the-clicks-node-on-the-screen-a-probability-is-the-clicks-frequency-over-records-c-itself-is-a-ruler-and-clock-reading-a-unit-conversion-record-191-the-consequences-an-experiment-produces-its-observable-by-a-detector-declared-in-the-world-file-never-by-reading-the-hosts-state-the-registers-pins-as-detector-readings-record-163-5-the-comparison-of-the-law-with-nature-goes-through-the-transformation-in-both-directions-and-a-board-quantity-is-never-compared-with-nature-directly-the-dictionary-is-completed-by-the-derivation-mathematician-as-the-targets-close-a-row-per-observed-quantity-its-reading-and-its-section)); the dictionary, one row per observed
  value, in [HIGHLIGHTS 5.7, "The transformation from the GameBoard to the observed values"](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#57-from-the-world-we-see-to-the-vector-world-the-conversions-the-principles-what-is-derived-and-what-is-input-2026-09-21).
  "after the detector, name the vector": scalars, integer 3-vectors in
  declared units, the traceless second-moment tensor **T**, the record's
  phase-count vector **f**, "the only kind reality has"; "a formula
  gives, a run proves" ([record
