- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-21: "how
  should one see everything, given that the world is converted for us
  through a detector? in vector and tensor terms? this is what Einstein
  was missing, it seems to me" ([record 196](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); "update the Highlights
  immediately ...: mainly how the conversions work and why, from the
  physical world we see to the world of vectors" ([record 198](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#198-highlights-57-written-on-the-owners-order-the-owner-2026-09-21-about-0215z-translated-so-everything-is-closed-on-your-side-update-the-highlights-immediately-in-git-on-the-system-mainly-how-the-conversions-work-and-why-from-the-physical-world-we-see-to-the-world-of-vectors-the-principles-what-is-derived-and-what-is-needed-and-why-the-bosss-answer-every-decision-of-the-day-is-recorded-and-the-builds-are-in-flight-the-one-item-open-on-the-owners-side-is-the-seventh-verb-record-197-highlightsmd-section-57-written-the-statement-record-181-the-three-conversions-a-thing-to-a-vector-the-world-to-a-reading-the-moments-as-the-stress-energy-tensor-a-reading-to-a-bit-the-gram-form-and-the-threshold-the-six-operations-and-the-two-non-linear-places-the-six-principles-locality-straightness-and-one-pace-with-c-derived-nothing-at-a-node-and-nothing-discarded-rows-and-bodies-with-one-motion-primitive-equations-first-the-generic-vector-form-first-what-is-derived-and-what-is-not-the-three-kinds-of-input-with-why-and-the-list-of-what-is-decided-and-in-build-with-the-one-open-question), Highlights
  5.7); "we must understand what in our world, after the detector,
  connects to a vector value and in what form"; "everything a formula can
  give, let it give; in a run, only prove" ([record 205](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); "the values we
  see in the world are behind a detector, including distance, speed and

### 20. 2026-09-21, 04:30Z to 05:55Z: Lorentz, A and B, then B replaced: the law stays the six verbs and states its FAIL row; the Lorentz factor is a root, the seventh verb, not admitted; the covariant readings keep the exact square as an integer

### 21. 2026-09-21 to 2026-09-22: the clock's word is the age moment; the one wall function of the crowd; the working bound is never a register; Newton and Poisson after a detector

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A clock in a gravitational
  potential (the redshift, the GPS term, Pound and Rebka); the potential
  1 / r against the flux 1 / r^2; Newton's fall and Poisson's equation
  as measured things; the register of a computer.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** The clock's word: what a body's
  clock counts is the age moment a_tau, the sum of amount x age over the
  rays at its Node, "the law's default word ... under the identity
  clock-age-v1; in the paper it is an assumption (P9), not a derivation"
  ([record 394](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#394-the-owners-decision-the-clocks-word-is-the-age-word-the-age-moment-as-the-laws-default-entered-in-the-paper-as-an-assumption-2026-09-21-1400z-and-140108z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-1422z-the-owner-to-the-visualiser-the-visualisers-transcript-140108z-translated-verbatim-choose-the-age-word-pass-it-to-the-boss-the-question-in-front-of-him-page-13-of-the-gallery-and-docsdesignsclock_agenotemd-what-a-bodys-clock-counts-as-the-laws-default-the-presence-a_r-or-the-age-moment-a_tau-his-questions-before-the-word-1356z-to-1359z-what-is-this-explain-where-does-the-difference-come-from-is-this-only-for-specific-experiments-answered-by-the-visualiser-on-the-gameboard-on-a-fan-line-the-beam-does-not-dilute-so-the-presence-is-4-f-at-both-distances-while-the-age-on-the-row-is-the-distance-22-f-42-f-in-the-shell-mean-the-presence-is-1--r2-the-force-and-the-age-moment-1--r-the-retarded-potential-derivations_beam-51-natures-clocks-following-the-potential-gps-457-microseconds-per-day-against-283-under-the-presence-galileo-0333-against-0631-1907-against-2000-from-the-integer-dwelling-ages-11-and-21-the-word-is-the-laws-default-everywhere-deciding-only-clocks-inside-crowds-the-push-the-flight-and-the-release-unchanged-the-owner-to-the-paper-coordinator-about-1400z-translated-i-said-the-age-moment-but-this-is-added-to-the-paper-as-an-assumption-right-the-decision-as-the-boss-reads-both-the-age-moment-is-the-laws-default-word-under-the-identity-clock-age-v1-as-notemd-section-7-orders-the-work-the-default-of-count_component-beam_law-section-3-step-5-enginemd-the-catalogs-clock-row-the-readings-that-move-re-registered-with-the-identity-on-their-lines-series-es-scalar-world-the-g2-gravity-crowds-series-u-and-v-the-catalogs-neutron-star-the-bound-clock-of-record-123-series-t-carries-both-words-and-does-not-move-in-the-paper-it-is-an-assumption-p9-not-a-derivation-the-alternative-kept-with-what-refutes-it-on-the-form-the-papers-wave-22-head-d83bb191-pr-opened-by-the-boss-at-1420z-already-carries-it-so-the-build-assigned-to-the-g2-session-under-record-392s-convergence-the-engines-default-must-follow-the-papers-assumption-or-the-paper-describes-an-engine-that-does-not-exist-g2s-answer-to-the-owners-140359z-question-does-it-affect-a-galaxy-clusters-clock-on-the-form-yes-on-the-scale-no-a-cluster-members-clock-term-g-m--r-c2-about-1e-5-the-dispersion-1e-3-new-under-the-age-word-the-core-redder-than-the-outskirts-by-about-10-kms-measured-in-clusters-wojtak-et-al-2011-sadeh-et-al-2015-one-sided-a-second-confirmed-prediction-not-dark-matter-and-to-141008z-does-this-need-updating-the-registered-readings-that-move-the-three-designs-sections-under-the-age-word-a-mapping-note-all-folded-into-the-build-order), the owner: "Choose the age word; pass it to the Boss";
  "this is added to the paper as an assumption, right?"). The generic
  solution: "not a rule for light but one rule for every accumulator: the
  crowd of other numbers at a Node stretches the wall of every accumulator
  by the same factor, the wall x (d + a_tau n) against the rate x d"
  ([record 421](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#421-the-owners-word-what-is-the-generic-solution-always-ask-yourself-this-2026-09-21-153910z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-1539z-the-owner-translated-what-is-the-generic-solution-always-ask-yourself-this-the-bosss-answer-from-the-tree-issue-605-one-wall-v1-record-410-not-a-rule-for-light-but-one-rule-for-every-accumulator-the-crowd-of-other-numbers-at-a-node-stretches-the-wall-of-every-accumulator-by-the-same-factor-the-wall-x-d--a_tau-n-against-the-rate-x-d-for-a-bodys-clock-that-is-todays-owed-count-with-the-same-integers-for-a-rows-flight-it-is-new-the-rays-pace-c_h--1--a_tau-n--d-the-clocks-slowing-from-the-same-rows-which-is-the-time-half-of-the-bending-with-no-new-word-the-space-half-from-the-push-that-exists-for-bodies-acting-on-a-row-with-the-energy-as-the-weight-in-place-of-the-content-a-body-at-rest-e_0--q-s-m-the-same-number-a-ray-its-labels-magnitude-accumulated-in-a-vector-the-permutation-on-the-direction-index-moving-the-heading-to-the-fans-nearest-line-by-integer-comparisons-two-verbs-that-exist-no-family-name-no-declared-2-the-three-tests-passing-in-form-the-price-every-row-reading-at-every-node-the-pins-the-shapiro-delay-in-the-potentials-form-and-the-bending-in-two-halves-2-g-m--b-c2-from-the-wall-and-2-g-m--b-c2-from-the-push-einsteins-4-from-the-law-if-n-checks-map-shows-the-halves-sum-exactly-the-standing-rule-written-into-skillsboss-orchestratorskillmd-what-is-the-generic-solution-before-any-rule-for-one-family-any-declared-input-supplying-a-number-nature-gives-or-any-patch-beside-a-generic-step-the-boss-asks-and-answers-in-writing-what-the-generic-solution-is-a-declared-input-is-the-last-resort-named-a-patch-with-the-generic-candidate-that-would-replace-it-and-the-check-that-decides), the owner: "What is the generic solution? Always ask
  yourself this"): a count at the rate r against the wall d becomes the
  count at the rate r x d against the wall d x (d + c x a_tau x n), the

### 22. 2026-09-22, 04:50Z: a detector's clock is a member of the age wall's set at coefficient 1; the GameBoard holds the clock of every experiment

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A detector's own time; a
  measured duration, period or rate.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** "the clock is a thing on the
  GameBoard, a lamp at its declared rate or a body's own clock (the count
  of its self-creations, its age; the age moment the default word), and
  the detector reads its clicks; a measured duration, period or rate is
  the detector's reading of that clock, never the host's interval count
  on its own" ([record 678](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); "a detector asks nothing of its Node, which
  keeps nothing; it counts the intervals as every body does and the one
  wall function stretches that count by the age moment ... of the rows
  that meet it, the same wall that stretches a body's clock" ([record 709](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#709-the-owner-the-detectors-clock-is-the-clock-of-the-node-it-sits-on-send-its-relation-to-the-physicists-one-wall-2026-09-22-0450z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-0458z-the-owner-translated-check-again-whether-it-works-and-tell-me-again-after-thinking-and-that-it-is-a-truly-comprehensive-generic-and-correct-solution-send-but-check-how-it-connects-to-what-nature-the-chief-physicist-is-building-now-he-builds-a-clock-that-runs-inside-nodes-or-something-like-it-it-may-simply-be-a-different-version-of-this-think-a-moment-the-bosss-answer-it-works-and-it-is-the-same-mechanism-not-another-version-the-physicists-step-2-every-family-under-one-wall-is-the-one-wall-function-age_wall-stretching-by-the-crowd-at-a-node-the-accumulators-of-a-declared-set-measuredage_wall_set-today-the-bodys-own-clock-at-coefficient-1-clock-age-v1-and-the-rows-flight-at-1--gamma-optical-the-functions-own-note-says-a-member-joins-by-a-declaration-in-the-set-and-nothing-else-moving-the-owners-solution-is-a-third-member-the-detectors-time-counter-at-coefficient-1-like-a-bodys-clock-a-detector-that-is-a-body-already-counts-so-a-face-detector-which-has-no-body-keeps-one-owed-accumulator-per-face-node-from-the-crowd-there-and-its-click-writes-the-nodes-clock-checked-over-every-case-a-body-detector-unchanged-an-empty-face-c-measured-the-muons-faces-owes-0-so-the-nodes-clock-is-the-interval-count-and-the-tick-is-the-detectors-own-reading-a-detector-in-a-crowd-the-hubble-detector-among-six-masses-029-to-0995-per-interval-reads-in-its-slowed-clock-which-is-the-re-read-a-screen-of-many-nodes-has-a-clock-per-node-and-every-click-names-its-node-the-emitters-births-are-already-on-its-clock-under-the-wall-local-the-detector-reads-its-own-node-only-the-accumulator-the-detectors-own-record-an-external-entity-nothing-kept-at-a-node-no-root-two-counters-and-a-division-no-registered-number-of-an-empty-face-moves-the-owed-count-exactly-0-only-the-hubbles-which-should-one-honest-caveat-it-derives-nothing-it-applies-the-existing-clock-word-record-394-p9-in-the-paper-to-one-more-member-no-new-assumption-and-the-model-has-no-other-time-sent-1-the-chief-physicist-his-machinery-after-the-step-2-fold-and-merge-a-separate-pr-of-about-one-hour-the-detectors-counter-in-age_wall_set-at-coefficient-1-the-face-click-writing-the-nodes-clock-enginemds-two-rows-a-test-with-a-face-in-a-crowd-and-without-the-gate-sets-digests-unchanged-the-reviewer-reads-2-clock-reader-two-words-tier-b-of-the-audit-g-g2-j-w-restated-in-the-detectors-clock-from-the-registered-records-the-pins-restated-before-the-numbers-the-old-rows-history-about-four-hours-branch-clock-rereads-3-label-fixer-already-carries-the-right-sentences-for-c-and-the-muon-4-the-highlights-line-below),

### 23. 2026-09-22, 04:51Z to 04:59Z: the click theorem: a click is the passage of information Node to Node; a click family's transformations are the Lorentz group up to scale; Lorentz is assumed of the world and shown to be brought by the click

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** A velocity, a detector's clock
  in motion, the Lorentz transformation, Bondi's k-calculus (the one-way
  Doppler factor k), E = m c^2 and the dispersion of a massive particle.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** Two worlds, defined: "The
  GameBoard: Nodes on the cubic lattice, integer rows on them, and the
  tick, the interval's count, which no detector ever reads. The real
  world: detectors and their clicks only." A click is a triple, "(the
  Node, `n_D` at the arrival, what arrived)", `n_D` the detector's own
  count; a click family `F_D` the set of clicks of one detector and its
  six neighbours; "a velocity is a ratio of two integers and never a
  reading of the tick" (the click frame (PR #769), section 0,
  "Definitions", lines 17 to 40). The two assumptions: "(A1) A click is
  the passage of information from Node to Node, at most one Node per
  interval ... (A2) A click's content is an amplitude with a phase that
  splits each interval between staying and hopping, the mass the staying
  share" (lines 41 to 69; beside them, since the owner's word of record
  799, LOCALITY OUTSIDE, "every Outside passage is a chain of clicks
  between neighbouring places ... It is A1 read from above, and it
  FOLLOWS FROM A1 ALONE together with the definition of Outside", the
  pair's click the one exception, the same lines). The theorem: "let G
  be the set of
  transformations between click families that preserve the conversion's
  structure ...; G is the conversion's symmetry. Then: (i) G is a group,
  and with (A1) alone it is the Lorentz group up to scale: in one
  dimension the maps (u, w) -> (k u, w / k) ..., the one-parameter group
  of boosts with the invariant u w = t^2 - x^2, whose parameter is the
  one-way factor k ...; in three dimensions the Lorentz group SO(1, 3) up
  to scale and translation, by the theorem of Alexandrov and Zeeman; (ii)
  With (A2) the group acts on the amplitudes as the covariance of the
  discrete-time Dirac walk ...; (iii) The lattice's own symmetries are
  the 48 ..., which contain no boost" (lines 70 to 109). The proof's
  k-calculus, Bondi's three relations: k = sqrt((1 + v) / (1 - v)), v =
  (k^2 - 1) / (k^2 + 1), gamma = (k + 1 / k) / 2, "the boosts compose by
  k_1 k_2 (a one-parameter abelian group, SO(1, 1))" (lines 110 to 173,
  item (b)); these are the proof's own formulas, not "the three
  formulas" of the owner's word of 05:58Z, which are entry 25's. Over Z
  the only cone-preserving bijections are the 48; over Q, G is the
  boosts with rational k, dense in SO(1, 1), the Lorentz group up to
  scale its closure (item (a)). The verdict: "LORENTZ FROM THE CLICKS
  GIVEN the line k_AB = k_BA (the two directions alike, the relativity
  principle), which is r^2 = 1 - v^2 for the moving record's own count;
  NOT WITHOUT IT"; and of the law as built: it meets (A1) and not (A2),
  "its hop being a whole-record schedule with no staying amplitude"
  (lines 174 to 218).
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-22, by
  voice: "we do not need that wall at all; one only needs to define what
  our real world is ...; a detector's velocity is a click that passes
  information to the detector beside it ...; the theorem the mathematician
  must show in modern algebra is very simple: that the way we arranged
  the board brings, in the real world, the clicks to Lorentz; then there
  is nothing to prove, because the real world is known to behave like
  Lorentz, and that we assume; our only assumptions are what a click is"
  ([record   [decisions of 2026-09-21](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-21)
  (the owner's word of 2026-09-22): "A click is the passage of
  information from Node to Node ...; reality is the clicks; Lorentz is
  the symmetry of the clicks, assumed of the world and required of the
  board"; the theorem itself, the click frame (PR #769), section 0,
  lines 8 to 218, at `1dd81fe`, on `main` since PR #769 merged at
  `70e9781a`.
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** Not in the code, by the
  owner's word ("no code"): the theorem is a statement about the clicks
  and lives in the click frame (PR #769). What the code has of its
  hypotheses: (A1), one Node per interval at most, `nature_beam.py:783`
  (`Flight.accumulator`, the rate 2 S_1 Q against the wall 2 T_D) and
  `engine.py:119` (`step_axis`, `at_most` 1); the detector's count,
  entry 22; (A2) not met on main (the click frame, section 0, lines 174
  to 181; parts 4 and 5, sections 5 and 6), a separate hypothesis not
  opened.
- **(e) The reading.** What decides is "the ratio of the two one-way
  Doppler factors, read at the ground through records; its pins are in
  section 3" (the click frame, lines 201 to 218); "k_AB NOT READ, the
  missing direction" (section 4, lines 348 to 404; the Inside | Outside
  table, row 3). Read today: the round trip and z under the law and the
  identity (NATURE rows 4a, 4b; series S, entry 20). Nothing in the
  register yet reads the one-way factor.
- **(f) Superseded.** The wall and the declared identity as the road to
  Lorentz ("no wall is needed", [record 745](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)): record 270's identity
  "becomes unnecessary for the reading" and stays beside the law as a
  hypothesis; the engine meeting (A2) "is a separate hypothesis not
  opened" (the Highlights line of record 749).

### 24. 2026-09-22, 05:02Z to 05:41Z: the paper's framing: Inside and Outside; "matches nature", never "is nature"; whatever can be computed algebraically is computed algebraically; Inside formulas and Outside formulas; the conversion as a map

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The passage from the lattice
  and its momentum to the motion, the energy, the clock rate, the fall and
  the orbit that a detector measures; the quantum level, where the
  physics has no equation of passage from the state to the outcome.
- **(b) The object in the algebra.** The framing: "inside the board we
  propagate as a beam, with our beam's formulas, algebraic formulas, and
  above the board we pass with amplitudes to above the GameBoard, which
  is our reality ...; we do not claim that nature is so; we say that ...
  what we computed, matches the results in nature; ... we assume, it is
  an assumption, that the board cannot be measured in any other way ...;
  whatever can be computed algebraically, compute algebraically, because
  the board is a finite computable thing" ([record 762](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). The two words:
  "Inside is inside the board, where no one can measure; Outside is above
  the board ...; it is not reality, it is the game; we claim that Outside
  represents reality to a very high degree, and we must show it"; a
  detector's clock "simply emits and receives; ... the input it receives
  back is exactly the detector's time; ... a Node need not hold a
  detector's time at all" ([record 768](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). The method: "one only needs to
  take assumptions and show that under them the formulas come out; and
  where there are none, one can run experiments inside the board and rise
  above it; ... to do things inside the board you must push them from
  above, you cannot start them inside; ... the paper must be more modern
  algebra and fewer experiments; ... it is not exactly a proof, it simply
  shows that this is Lorentz" ([record 772](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#772-the-owner-the-same-way-for-newton-and-more-assumptions-then-the-formulas-outside-what-is-done-inside-is-pushed-from-outside-the-clock-confirmed-a-pulse-and-its-return-the-paper-more-modern-algebra-and-fewer-experiments-sync-the-highlights-2026-09-22-0520z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-0520z-the-owners-words-by-voice-if-you-got-lorentz-above-the-board-from-the-clicks-you-will-surely-get-newton-too-and-perhaps-more-things-above-the-board-the-same-way-we-showed-lorentz-above-the-board-one-can-show-newton-many-things-one-only-needs-to-take-assumptions-and-show-that-under-them-the-formulas-come-out-and-where-there-are-none-one-can-run-experiments-inside-the-board-and-rise-above-it-our-strength-is-that-you-can-show-results-above-the-board-when-you-do-things-inside-the-board-but-to-do-things-inside-the-board-you-must-push-them-from-above-you-cannot-start-them-inside-confirm-the-clock-a-clock-measures-only-a-detector-by-giving-a-pulse-and-when-the-pulse-returns-that-is-the-detectors-clock-all-this-algebraic-mathematics-only-needs-to-be-shown-in-the-paper-the-paper-must-be-more-modern-algebra-and-fewer-experiments-send-an-agent-or-the-boss-to-sync-everything-said-now-into-the-highlights-everything-said-on-lorentz-with-the-demonstrations-the-algebraic-demonstration-clear-it-is-not-exactly-a-proof-it-simply-shows-that-this-is-lorentz-and-we-proceed-from-there-the-boss-the-clock-confirmed-a-detectors-clock-is-a-pulse-it-gives-and-the-return-of-that-pulse-on-its-own-record-record-768-the-highlights-synced-by-the-boss-himself-one-new-line-below-for-the-method-assumptions-then-the-formulas-outside-experiments-inside-rise-outside-where-no-formula-the-initial-state-pushed-from-outside-the-paper-more-modern-algebra-fewer-experiments-the-click-theorem-shows-that-it-is-lorentz-it-does-not-prove-lorentz-the-lines-of-records-749-762-and-768-already-on-main-and-the-click-theorems-own-line-to-follow-at-pr-769s-merge-with-its-sha-and-its-verdicts-the-mathematician-ordered-part-7-newton-outside-the-same-way-the-assumptions-the-formula-the-registers-d3-as-the-check-after-part-6-the-writer-told-to-prepare-not-apply-the-papers-shape-under-more-modern-algebra-fewer-experiments-which-experiment-rows-move-to-the-register-and-which-algebra-sections-grow-the-cost-after-the-framing-list)). and corrected at `e26c1f43` on the
  reviewer's lines: row 1's unit chain, row 3's steps of the circle N,
  and the one Inside to Outside constant joining the clock's row to the
  fall's, delta k = (n S / d)(g h / c^2), nature's form times the
  declared n S / d, equal to nature's if and only if n S = d); "every Outside formula is a ratio or a
  difference of counts at a click, never the tick"; "the inverse map
  exists on every row but the click's" (the same, the two closing facts).
  The conversion as a map, in the algebra's words (section 9, lines 1178
  to 1364): Phi: I -> O is a composite of four maps: (a) the pointer ev:
  Z[Z_N] -> Z[zeta_N], "a surjective RING HOMOMORPHISM, exactly. Its
  kernel is the ideal generated by x^(N/2) + 1, which is the merge's
  cancel ...: the rows modulo the cancel ARE the cyclotomic integers"
  (the first isomorphism theorem, exact; lines 1238 to 1258), "FOR ev AT
  THE EXACT ROOT", the paper's object: on the engine's rounded tables C
  and S at the scale 256 the evaluation is a Z-linear map that "is NOT a
  ring homomorphism ..., whose kernel contains the cancel ... and is
  larger than it", so on the engine the relation holds "within the
  tables' rounding" (the same lines; entry 17 (f) says the same of the
  several-arm form); (b) the
  weight R(f) = |ev f|^2, "a POSITIVE QUADRATIC FORM on the lattice, and
  equivalently a PURE STATE of the group algebra, exactly": R(f) =
  chi_1(f* f), chi_1 a character of C[Z_N], the GNS space C and the
  pointer the GNS vector; the lattice Gleason as "the admissible states
  are the positive combinations of the odd characters" (lines 1259 to
  1294), the name fitting "exactly for the paper's object, chi_1 at the
  exact root", while "the engine realizes it within the tables'
  rounding", one part in 256 on a single row and larger where the
  element nearly cancels, the rotation invariance likewise up to the
  rounding (the same lines); (c) the rung, "a THRESHOLD, the one non-linear read-out, and in
  the probability's words a STOCHASTIC MAP (a Markov kernel) ..., exact
  within the wheel's grain 1 / N"; "This step deletes the record (P6): it
  is not a morphism of Inside" (lines 1295 to 1309); (d) the count map
  on the hop, (Nodes apart, counts apart) -> Q, "a REPRESENTATION of the
  counts' monoid in Q by ratios in the mean", on which the Lorentz group
  up to scale acts exactly over Q, and on the amplitudes only up to
  corrections of order m^2 v^2 (lines 1310 to 1321). What does not fit:
  "Outside is Inside modulo a kernel" for the whole conversion, and "a
  functor between two categories" (the table at lines 1322 to 1333).
  The statement of record 777 in the map's words: "on the amplitude,
  Outside = rung o (chi_1 of f* f) o ev on the record's group-ring
  element ...; on the schedule, Outside = (Nodes apart) / (counts apart)
  in Q, on which the Lorentz group up to scale acts; the two sides meet
  at the detector's own count, which is the age wall's member at
  coefficient 1 ...; what the algebra does not already have is the
  composite, and that is the law's own, the click" (lines 1353 to 1364).

### 25. 2026-09-22, 05:45Z to 06:20Z: the three formulas: the step beneath the board, the step above it, and the conversion; a transformation for every formula with the detector at the place; locality Outside; the paper's foundation

- **(a) The thing in ordinary physics.** The equation of motion as
  Newton and Einstein wrote it; the equations of light; a velocity in
  the real world; what we are made of, in the paper's foundation.

  E^2 + 3 p is the formula of a step beneath the board; one needs the

  least separation of two clicks one Link, the least thing a detector

  ratio 1 / k ([record 803](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#803-the-owner-the-step-above-the-board-as-a-formula-the-minimal-distance-between-a-click-and-a-click-2026-09-22-0620z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-0611z-the-owners-words-by-voice-we-have-the-step-formula-beneath-the-board-what-is-interesting-is-the-step-formula-above-it-what-is-the-minimal-distance-between-a-click-and-a-click-very-interesting-we-have-the-step-below-and-the-step-above-is-also-interesting-as-a-formula-the-bosss-reading-to-be-checked-the-outside-step-has-its-own-quantum-as-the-inside-step-has-one-interval-and-one-node-in-place-the-least-separation-of-two-clicks-read-as-two-places-is-one-link-two-detectors-at-neighbouring-nodes-a1-in-the-detectors-own-time-the-least-thing-a-detector-times-is-a-pulse-to-its-neighbour-and-its-return-record-768-two-intervals-inside-the-detectors-own-unit-outside-the-tick-itself-gameboard-and-never-read-for-a-moving-record-one-node-per-hop-and-k-counts-between-hops-v--1k-so-the-outside-velocities-are-ratios-m3-over-q-with-the-quantum-of-velocity-between-1k-and-1k--1-and-c-outside-one-link-per-the-least-count-the-outside-step-formula-is-then-place_j1---place_j-and-count_j1---count_j-for-the-next-click-of-the-same-record-carried-from-the-inside-steps-one-interval-and-one-node-by-the-conversion-the-k-factors-the-rate-r-newtons-and-einsteins-step-is-the-continuum-limit-of-that-quantized-step-ordered-as-one-clause-to-the-derivation-mathematician-for-part-8s-opening-the-outside-step-with-its-quantum-trig_01rdfn57b4kfewe5bsmqrszi-in-the-last-commits-on-click-frame-or-first-on-click-frame-2-and-to-the-einstein-mathematician-for-his-outside-step-of-a-body-and-a-clock-trig_01yv9sbn4c9mr1kznfwuaec1-what-is-a-theorem-of-a1-and-the-conversion-and-what-needs-a2-or-an-assumption-the-registers-readings-by-kind-that-show-a-least-step-ss-face-clicks-369-and-345-ts-arrival-counts-the-muons-70-and-124-ws-first-click-ages-815-876-876-d3s-births-at-one-node-or-not-read)). And the foundation, in the owner's words:
  "in the real world we are built of clicks, and to move a click from
  place to place one must put it into the board and then take it out of
  the board; that is motion in the real world, but the transformation
  must be done there; ... broadly the basis is Inside, and we are
  operated Outside, by an emitter or clicks" ([record 802](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md); "a
  mathematician should check that it is right and fits", the same
  record).
- **(c) When, and by whose word.** The model owner, 2026-09-22, by voice
  ([record 787](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 788](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 799](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 802](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 803](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md));
  the Highlights lines [decisions of 2026-09-21](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#decisions-of-2026-09-21)
  (the owner's words of 2026-09-22): "Every Outside formula carries its
  own transformation, and a reading at another place is a detector at
  that place", "Three formulas: the step beneath the board, the step
  above it, and the conversion between them; from these everything is
  computed, without code", "Locality Outside too", "Highlights 5.4
  carries no line that contradicts the current law ...; every agent cuts
  from its own file what no longer applies" (with the foundation
  sentence). The Boss's relay of record 806, not on main at this head:
  one basis, Inside; the step above is derived, never set beside it.
- **(d) Where it lives in the code on main.** Not in the code, by the
  owner's word (entry 23): (I) is the interval's map, `nature_beam.py:3299`
  (`nature_beam`), with its verbs at entry 16's places and its invariant
  under the identity at `world.py:475` and `engine.py:182`; (II) and
  (III) are derivations, the click frame (PR #769) and the two files
  ordered in record 787, `docs/designs/light_outside/DERIVATION.md` on
  the branch `light-outside` and `docs/designs/einstein_outside/DERIVATION.md`
  on the branch `einstein-outside`, neither on main at this head.
- **(e) The reading.** Every Outside formula reached this way is checked
  against the register's readings by kind before any run ([record 788](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md));
  what is read today is the Inside | Outside table's last column (entry
  24); the equations of light and of Einstein derived Outside are NOT
  READ yet, their files in flight.
- **(f) Superseded.** The wall and the declared identity as the road to
  Lorentz (entry 23); the forty-page and the referee's paper (entry 24).
  Nothing of the law. The pruning rule of record 802 applies to this
  file as to every agent's: every entry above whose object was later
  replaced says so in its own row (f), and the argument below uses the
  current law's objects only.

## The physical quantities, when each entered, where it lives, and which of the six verbs act on it

The owner's word to the paper's writer, relayed by the Boss on
2026-09-22: "the road from basic physics to the algebra must say how
mass, charge and the rest entered it and which verbs act on them." One
row per quantity as the paper defines it. The verbs are numbered as
[HIGHLIGHTS 5.7, "The six operations"](../../HIGHLIGHTS.md#57-from-the-world-we-see-to-the-vector-world-the-conversions-the-principles-what-is-derived-and-what-is-input-2026-09-21)
lists them: (1) the translation of an accumulator by its rate; (2) the
bilinear form with a declared matrix; (3) the group-ring addition; (4)
the permutation; (5) the evaluation at the primitive N-th root of unity;
(6) the Euclidean division with the remainder kept, the carry that is
the event and the threshold. A quantity a verb only reads is named so.
Every `file:line` is on `main` (the bases above); a record is of the log
of the date its link names.

## The argument in order

**1. The measured quantities are counts at clicks and their ratios, so
the Outside is arithmetic in Q.** The foundation, in the owner's words,
stated as his and to be checked by a mathematician ([record 802](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md); the
Highlights line of that record): "in the real world we are built of
clicks; to move a click from place to place one puts it into the board
and takes it out; that is motion in the real world, and the
transformation must be done there; the basis is Inside, and we are
operated Outside, by emitters and clicks." Locality holds Outside too: a
packet passes from place to place and never jumps, a velocity in the real
world is a click passing information to the neighbouring Node
([record 799](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md); the click theorem's (A1) read from above, entry 25). From
2026-09-20 the law has two kinds
of readings and one rule about them: a comparison with nature is between
detector readings and nature's laws, never between the GameBoard's rules
and nature's laws ([record 20](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 26](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); only a detector's reading is
a measurement ([record 281](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); no experiment reads the GameBoard, every
external entity is a detector or an emitter ([record 562](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); the GameBoard
holds the clock of every experiment, and a detector's clock is a pulse
and its return on its own record ([record 678](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 768](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). A click is a
triple, the Node, the detector's own count at the arrival, and what
arrived; a click family's counts add, so their ratios lie in Q, and "a
velocity is a ratio of two integers and never a reading of the tick" (the
click frame (PR #769), section 0, lines 17 to 40). Every Outside formula
of the Inside | Outside table "is a ratio or a difference of counts at a
click, never the tick" (section 7 (3)). That is why the dictionary of
HIGHLIGHTS 5.7 has one row per observed value and each row names the
counting that produces it (entry 19): a velocity Outside is Nodes apart
over counts apart between two clicks of neighbouring detectors (entries
23 and 25; [record 799](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)), and what a moving reader counts, 1 +- v / c, is
the Doppler factor (entry 14); a time is a body's own turn count; a
probability is a click's frequency over records (entry 12); c's value, 1
/ sqrt 3 Links per interval, is derived Inside as the norm of the flight
operator and read Outside by series Q's escapes against the rows' age
and by the cone (entry 18), and only its metres per second is a unit
conversion. Ordinary physics
behaves like arithmetic on the Outside because the Outside, by the law's
own rule, consists of nothing but integer counts and their ratios.

**2. The law Inside is integer and local: the six verbs and the three
tests.** On 2026-09-19 the owner's assumption was that "Everything is
derived from vector operations and the like" and that a Node keeps no
register (entry 5); on 2026-09-20 every count became an accumulator on
its reader's own record, s <- s + r; e <- [s >= d]; s <- s - e d, the
remainder kept (entry 13); on 2026-09-21 the inventory of every rule
found each one of six operations, and the owner decided the statement:
"the system becomes vectors, tensors and operations on them", one map
**F** per interval at every Node, the translation by a rate, the bilinear
form with a declared matrix, the group-ring addition, the permutation,
the evaluation at the primitive N-th root of unity, and the Euclidean
division with the remainder kept (entry 16). A rule enters the law only
if it is generic (one primitive, no family name), vector (one of the six
verbs, no root, no float) and local (its own record and the six
neighbours, nothing at a Node) (entry 16, [record 202](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). The consequence
for the paper is that the Inside is a finite algebraic object with a
closed form wherever a rate is constant, s(t) = floor(s_0 + r t), and an
exact iteration wherever a rate reads the state; "whatever can be
computed algebraically is computed algebraically, because the board is a
finite computable thing" ([record 762](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). The Inside behaves like modern
algebra because it is made of nothing else: integers on tori, one
piecewise-linear map, a group of 48 signed axis permutations under which
every rule is covariant, and a group ring Z[Z_N] for the phase.

**3. The couplings are tables and the Born rule is a coupling.** A force
is not a code path: it is a column with a sign and a lifetime on its
family, and the coupling is one signed inner product over the columns,
the bilinear form **C a** of a declared matrix of bounded integer ratios
against the label flow (entries 3, 7, 9). Born's rule is a coupling of the
same kind: the one bilinear form on the record's phase-count vector with
the Gram matrix of the tables computed from N alone, **f**^T **G f**,
whose uniqueness under the rotation of the phase, the balanced
splitter's conservation and non-negativity is the lattice Gleason
theorem, and one threshold, the rung (entry 17; its history, entries 2
and 4, is superseded and cited there as such). The declared
inputs are of three kinds only, the grain (N, Q, P, W, K and the
roundings at load), the family table with the width S, and the state and
apparatus (HIGHLIGHTS 5.7, "What is input, and why"; [record 189](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); the
masses are amounts on the non-compact scale and no number of the law
selects them, while what lives on a compact group is quantised (entry
11). So where the paper writes a coupling it writes a table, and where it
writes a probability it writes a quadratic form with one imported
constant, c_1 = 1, the choice of one pure state of the group algebra (the
click frame (PR #769), section 9, lines 1259 to 1294).

**4. Where the structure-preserving maps come from.** The paper's
formulas are three (the owner, [record 788](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), entry 25): the step beneath
the board, the Inside step, one interval of a record by the six verbs
with W = E'_0^2 + 3 **p** . **p** its invariant; the step above the
board, the Outside step, the equation of motion as Newton and Einstein
wrote it, a theorem and the image of the first under the third; and the
conversion between them, the emitter-to-click map, one transformation
per formula with a detector at every place read ([record 787](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). The
conversion's symmetry is not put in; the transformations between click
families are the symmetry of the conversion the two assumptions define. With (A1) alone, a click passes
information at most one Node per interval, the cone-preserving Q-linear
maps of the count pairs form a group, in one dimension the boosts (u, w)
-> (k u, w / k) with the invariant t^2 - x^2, the parameter Bondi's
one-way factor k of the proof's k-calculus; in three dimensions the Lorentz group up to
scale and translation by the theorem of Alexandrov and Zeeman; with (A2),
an amplitude with phase splitting between staying and hopping, the group
acts on the amplitudes as the Dirac walk's covariance to second order,
which fixes the moving detector's own rate at sqrt(1 - v^2) up to a
correction of relative order m^2 v^2, m the mass angle (entry 23; the
click frame (PR #769), section 0, lines 70 to 173). The lattice's own
group, the 48, contains no boost, so the maps act on the Outside and not
on the Inside, and the lattice differs from the world in direction from
the fourth order on. Lorentz of the real world is assumed and the
theorem shows how a click brings it, "it is not exactly a proof, it
simply shows that this is Lorentz" ([record 772](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); the same road serves
Newton (the fall and Kepler's ratio after a detector, rows 8 and 9 of the
Inside | Outside table, series D3) and the clock's field (rows 4 and 7,
series T and X). The whole conversion Inside -> Outside is a composite
the algebra names step by step, a ring homomorphism whose kernel is the
cancel and a positive quadratic form that is a pure state, both exact at
the exact root and realized on the engine within the tables' rounding,
a stochastic map exact within 1 / N, and a representation of the counts in Q on which the
Lorentz group up to scale acts; what has no name in the algebra is the
composite itself, the click (entry 24).

**5. Therefore the paper's results are identities of the algebra checked
against the register, not formulas chosen to fit.** Every formula the
paper states is either exact on the state (the conservation of the
books, Gauss's law under its world condition, the pace of every
direction, the click's form, the marginals of a pair, S(N) as an exact
function of the grain), a limit of the grain (c = 1 / sqrt 3, Born's
rule, Tsirelson's bound, Young's spacing) or a limit under an average
that covers the shell (Newton's and Coulomb's inverse square, the
retarded potential, Poisson's equation), and it says which
(`paper/general_formula/main.tex`, the abstract; every formula's rung,
[record 616](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md#616-the-owner-yes-to-all-a-new-reviewer-for-the-cut-the-paper-writer-archived-when-his-role-is-done-2026-09-22-0113z-recorded-by-the-boss-at-0115z-the-owner-in-his-own-words-translated-yes-to-all-a-new-reviewer-for-the-cut-and-cancel-the-paper-writer-if-he-has-finished-his-role-all-is-the-three-yesno-items-of-the-bosss-list-of-0100z-record-606-2-the-highlights-line-on-the-derivations-ground-records-605-607-609-every-formula-of-the-law-carries-its-ground-exact-on-the-state-a-limit-with-its-rate-or-a-limit-under-an-average-that-covers-the-shell-with-its-ripple-and-a-detector-reading-never-lifts-the-third-to-the-first-written-now-4-the-tag-paper-2026-09-22-on-the-merge-commit-of-the-cuts-pr-set-by-the-boss-at-the-merge-6-a-new-reviewer-session-for-the-cut-opened-with-the-cuts-state-when-the-coordinators-pr-opens-record-578-its-verdict-one-word-with-corrections-the-paper-writer-the-coordinator-session_01299yiyfeomygyrnalemzer-is-archived-when-his-role-is-done-the-boss-reads-that-as-the-merge-of-the-cuts-pr-with-the-reviewers-corrections-folded-since-the-corrections-need-the-writer-not-before-told-to-the-coordinator-at-0114z-trig_01memd9qcv3wm7xuhfcwgqjj-his-report-on-the-cut-must-therefore-carry-everything-the-heads-full-sha-the-page-count-as-compiled-the-six-lines-every-central-formula-with-its-status-and-rung-the-open-questions-with-their-distance-and-his-session-ends-at-the-merge-pr-709-records-611-to-614-merged-at-1c51a627-before-this-record-the-branch-restarted-with-record-615-carried)). The order of work since 2026-09-21 is that the formula
gives and the run proves: a number is derived from the six verbs before
any run and registered with its derivation beside it, and a run that
differs refutes ([record 176](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 205](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)). The register's rows are read
under one parameter set with nothing tuned after a verdict, and a FAIL
stays a FAIL in the same font as a PASS (NATURE, rules (a) and (e)): the
muon's dilation and the moving lamp's z fail under the law (rows 4a,
4b), the decay's curve and the binding's ratio fail (rows 8a, 7b),
Bell's S, the Mach-Zehnder, Malus and the clock's field under the age
word pass (rows 1a, 2b, 9, 12). What the paper may therefore claim is
that the Outside computed from the Inside matches nature result by
result, never that nature is the board ([record 762](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md), [record 768](../../LOG_2026-09-20.md)); and
what it must explain, by this history, is that the match is not a fit
because there was nothing to fit: the transition from ordinary physics
to modern algebra was made once, decision by decision, on the dates
above, and after it the law had no formula left to choose, only
identities to check.
